Conclusion: 彩虹时空中的量子涨落修正取决于具体彩虹函数的形式，且该效应非常微小，但仍可通过高精度Casimir力实验对彩虹引力参数空间进行约束。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect in rainbow space-time, focusing on leading-order corrections to the Casimir energy and force. Starting with the scalar field Lagrangian in rainbow space-time, with parallel plates introduced through delta-function potentials, we find the corresponding energy-momentum tensor. We obtain the vacuum expectation value of this energy-momentum tensor by expressing it as a quadratic operator acting on the Green's function. By solving the Euler-Lagrange equation of a scalar field in rainbow space-time, we obtain the Green's function solutions. Employing these Green's function solutions in the vacuum expectation value of the energy-momentum tensor, we obtain the modified Casimir energy and Casimir force expressions in rainbow space-time. We study the variation of the deformed Casimir force and energy with the distance between the plates for different choices of rainbow functions. Our results show that for two choices of rainbow functions, the absolute value of the Casimir energy and force is decreasing or increasing, whereas for one specific choice of rainbow functions, it remains the same as the standard result in Minkowski space-time. Comparing our result with experimentally measured value of Casimir force, we obtain the bound on the rainbow parameter dependent terms to be of the order of 10^-24.

</details>


### [99] [A comparison of two constructions for dynamical corrections to Wald entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27732)
*Sayantani Bhattacharyya,Parthajit Biswas,Nilay Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了两种在一般高导数引力理论中将稳态黑洞的Wald熵扩展到非稳态情形的构造方法（$S_\text{Wall}$与$S_\text{dyn}$），阐明了两者的根本区别：$S_\text{Wall}$利用近视界几何的boost对称性构造且不要求线性化，而$S_\text{dyn}$基于Wald-Iyer Noether荷的协变相空间分析，仅适用于稳态解的线性微扰。作者证明由$S_\text{Wall}$给出$S_\text{dyn}$是直接的，反之则更微妙，并发展了在满足特定技术条件下从$S_\text{dyn}$得到$S_\text{Wall}$局部表达式的算法，最后以Riemann平方项高阶导数引力为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般高导数引力理论中，将Wald熵从稳态黑洞推广到非稳态（动力学）情形是一个具有根本重要性的问题。两种现有构造（基于近视界boost对称性的$S_\text{Wall}$和基于Noether荷协变相空间的$S_\text{dyn}$）方法论截然不同，但其技术结构和适用条件的关系此前尚未被系统澄清。作者旨在阐明这两种构造之间的根本差异，并建立从一个构造到另一个构造的明确转换机制。

Method: 作者通过比较两种构造的技术基础来揭示其差异：$S_\text{Wall}$无需线性化即能构造（尽管线性化对其作为熵的物理解释是必要的，且其构造天然包含动力学波动的高阶项），而$S_\text{dyn}$仅定义在线性化微扰层面。作者证明从$S_\text{Wall}$推导$S_\text{dyn}$是直接的，相反方向则需要更细致的分析。作者开发了一种算法，在满足特定技术条件下，能从已知的$S_\text{dyn}$表达式获得$S_\text{Wall}$的局部表达式，并在Riemann平方项高导数引力理论这一具体例子中进行了显式验证。

Result: 作者成功阐明了$S_\text{Wall}$与$S_\text{dyn}$两种构造的内在差异，给出了从$S_\text{dyn}$反推$S_\text{Wall}$的算法（需满足条件），并在Riemann平方理论的例子中验证了分析结论。

Conclusion: 该工作厘清了非稳态黑洞熵的两种构造框架之间的关系：从$S_\text{Wall}$到$S_\text{dyn}$路径简单直接，而反向推导需要更谨慎的处理，作者为此提供了系统的算法框架，并在具体高导数理论中予以验证，深化了对非稳态黑洞熵概念及其构造方法的理解。

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the differences and similarities between two recent constructions, which are distinct in their methodologies for extending the Wald entropy of stationary black holes to non-stationary situations in general higher-derivative gravity. One of them, denoted by $S_\text{Wall}$, is constructed by exploiting the boost symmetry of the near-horizon geometry, whereas the other, denoted by $S_\text{dyn}$, is obtained from a covariant phase-space analysis based on the Wald-Iyer Noether charge formalism. While $S_\text{dyn}$ is, by construction, defined only for linearized fluctuations around a stationary black hole solution, $S_\text{Wall}$ does not require such a linearization for its construction. Although the linearization is necessary to interpret $S_\text{Wall}$ as a well-defined notion of entropy, the construction itself naturally contains terms that are higher order in the dynamical fluctuations. By comparing the technical structures underlying the two constructions, we clarify the fundamental differences between the methods on which they are based. We demonstrate that while the construction of $S_\text{dyn}$ given the $S_\text{Wall}$ is straightforward, the converse is more subtle. We develop an algorithm to obtain a local expression for $S_\text{Wall}$ from a known expression for $S_\text{dyn}$ in a generic diffeomorphism-invariant theory of gravity, provided certain technical conditions are satisfied. We justify our analytical findings with explicit demonstrations in a particular case: the Riemann-squared example of the higher-derivative theory of gravity.

</details>


### [100] [Non-conformal obstructions to bubble expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27874)
*David Mateos,Mikel Sanchez-Garitaonandia,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶相变中非共形热力学下的膨胀气泡流体动力学，揭示了新的流体动力学障碍（可部分或完全消除本应允许的泡壁解）、一类名为"激波引爆"的新解，以及泡壁速度空间中的间隙，并指出这些效应会显著压缩流体动能预算，进而抑制引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对一阶相变气泡膨胀的研究多基于共形（袋模型）状态方程，但真实系统如QCD类理论是非共形的。非共形效应对气泡解空间、泡壁速度和引力波信号的影响尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 分析介于袋模型（电弱相变常用）与QCD类理论之间的一大类状态方程插值；以纯SU(3) Yang-Mills理论作为具体基准确定气泡解；使用公开代码构造气泡解；并通过改变物质场对几何的反作用在全息框架下实现两类极限间的插值。

Result: 发现了新的流体动力学障碍（出现在泡壁和流体流动中，可部分或完全消除解）；识别出新解类别"激波引爆"（在稀疏波中插入额外激波的普通引爆）；泡壁速度空间被显著约束并在不同膨胀机制间出现间隙；对QCD类理论所有引爆解（包括激波引爆）均被排除；这些效应可强有力地影响流体动能预算并抑制最有效的引力波构型。

Conclusion: 非共形动力学对精确建模相变及其引力波谱至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrodynamics of expanding bubbles in first-order phase transitions with non-conformal thermodynamics. We analyze a broad class of equations of state interpolating between bag-model descriptions, commonly used for electroweak transitions, and QCD-like theories. As a concrete benchmark, we determine the bubble solutions for pure SU(3) Yang-Mills theory. We uncover a new set of hydrodynamic obstructions to bubble expansion. These obstructions arise both at the bubble wall and along the fluid flow, and can partially or completely eliminate otherwise allowed solutions. We also identify a new class of solutions, which we dub "shocked detonations", consisting of ordinary detonations with an additional shock inserted in the rarefaction wave. As a consequence of these obstructions, the space of admissible bubble wall velocities is significantly constrained, with gaps appearing between different expansion regimes. For example, for QCD-like theories, all detonation solutions, including shocked detonations, are excluded. We show that these effects can strongly impact the kinetic energy budget of the fluid and, therefore, the resulting gravitational-wave signal, potentially suppressing the most efficient configurations. Our results highlight the importance of non-conformal dynamics for accurately modeling phase transitions and the resulting gravitational-wave spectrum. The code used to construct the bubble solutions is publicly available. For completeness, we also show how the interpolation between the bag-model and QCD-like limits can be realized holographically by varying the backreaction of matter fields on the geometry.

</details>


### [101] [A regulated zero-temperature construction of the Fundamental Modular Region in pure Yang--Mills theory and QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27896)
*Rodrigo Carmo Terin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出将基本模区域（FMR）表述为受调控的副本加权朗道规范在零温极限，并通过费米-坡普科夫（FP）算子主导的修正项实现了绝对朗道规范的定义，无需参数化FMR边界，可自然推广到完整的量子色动力学（QCD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规范场论中，朗道规范存在Gribov副本问题，即规范固定不唯一。传统上定义绝对朗道规范需要精确刻画基本模区域（FMR）的边界，这是一个极为复杂的几何问题。作者旨在绕过这一困难，通过一种新的构型构造方式直接实现绝对朗道规范。

Method: 作者将FMR公式化为受调控的副本加权朗道规范在零温度（β→∞）极限下的结果。在有限β下，测度局域化于绝对极小值，领头阶修正由逆FP算子的贡献主导。为提供计算验证，作者在小的SU(2)格点上采用群体退火（population annealing）和集体盆地跳跃（collective basin hopping）两种算法进行数值实现。

Result: 该构造成功地定义了绝对朗道规范而无需参数化FMR边界，并在小SU(2)基准上通过数值方法给出了计算实现。该框架自然扩展到完整QCD，并具备哈密顿量图像：可被解释为FMR上的规范固定真空波泛函。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种规避FMR边界参数化的绝对朗道规范构造方案，兼具理论简洁性与数值可行性，且可推广至QCD，为规范固定问题提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We formulate the Fundamental Modular Region (FMR) as the zero-temperature limit of regulated copy-weighted Landau gauges. At finite $β$, the measure localizes on absolute minima, and the leading correction is dominated by the inverse Faddeev--Popov (FP) operator. A small $SU(2)$ benchmark provides a computational realization through population annealing and collective basin hopping. Our construction defines absolute Landau gauge without parametrizing the FMR boundary and extends naturally to full quantum chromodynamics (QCD). It also admits a Hamiltonian interpretation in terms of the gauge-fixed vacuum wave functional on the FMR.

</details>


### [102] [Generalized Kazakov-Migdal Models on Graphs via Artin-Ihara $L$-function and Random Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27935)
*So Matsuura,Kazutoshi Ohta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Artin-Ihara L-函数引入Kazakov-Migdal(KM)型规范理论，统一了先前工作中提出的模型。利用群流形上的调和分析，将环图上的KM型模型重述为由Schur测度控制的随机配分模型，并在大N_c极限下精确求解基本表示，证明了Gross-Witten-Wadia相变恰好发生在Young图极限形状触碰允许表示空间边界时，并与玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在统一并精确定义基于图的KM型规范理论框架，揭示其与随机配分模型、Artin-Ihara L-函数的深层联系，并解释规范理论中的相变与组合对象之间的本质关联。

Method: 利用Artin-Ihara L-函数将KM型规范理论定义在图（graph）上；运用群流形上的调和分析将模型转化为Schur测度控制的随机配分模型；在大N_c极限下通过Young图的极限形状分析精确求解环图上的KM型模型；通过谱曲线导出液滴图像（droplet picture）建立特征值密度与Maya图密度之间的对应关系。

Result: 精确求解了环图上基本表示下的KM型模型，证明Gross-Witten-Wadia相变对应于Young图极限形状接触表示空间边界；揭示了该相变与Bose-Einstein凝聚的内在联系；发现强/弱耦合对偶具有组合解释（Young图与其互补图的交换），反映了Artin-Ihara L-函数的函数方程；建立了幺正矩阵特征值密度与随机配分Maya图密度之间的明确关系。

Conclusion: KM型图规范理论通过Artin-Ihara L-函数获得了统一描述，其精确解揭示了规范理论相变、随机Young图极限形状与Bose-Einstein凝聚之间的深刻联系，且强/弱耦合对偶来自L-函数的函数方程，展现了规范理论与组合数论结构的丰富交叉。

Abstract: We introduce Kazakov-Migdal (KM)-type gauge theories on graphs via the Artin-Ihara $L$-function, providing a unified description of the models proposed in prior works. Using harmonic analysis on the group manifold, we reformulate the KM-type model on the cycle graph as a random partition model governed by the Schur measure. We exactly solve the KM-type model on the cycle graph in the fundamental representation as the random partition model in the large $N_c$ limit and demonstrate that the Gross-Witten-Wadia phase transition occurs precisely when the limiting shape of the Young diagram touches the boundary of the allowed representation space. We further clarify that this phase transition is intimately related to Bose-Einstein condensation, and the strong/weak coupling duality possesses a natural combinatorial interpretation as the exchange between the Young diagram and its complement, reflecting the functional equation of the Artin-Ihara $L$-function. We also establish a definitive relationship between the eigenvalue density of the unitary matrix and the Maya diagram density of the random partitions by deriving a droplet picture from the spectral curve.

</details>


### [103] [Mega-Space Current Algebra and Green-Schwarz Geometry in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27988)
*Machiko Hatsuda,Shin Sasaki,Masaya Yata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在显式T-对偶框架下研究杂弦理论中的Lorentz联络与规范联络，通过将Poláček-Siegel方案推广至包含Lorentz和规范扇区的"mega-space"，构造了弦世界sheet电流代数，揭示了Bergshoeff--de Roo（BdR）型联络平行性，并将杂弦Chern-Simons结构几何嵌入广义vielbein与曲率中。此外，Green-Schwarz反常消除条件可以从弦论协变导数（"nachos"算符）的Jacobi恒等式自然导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂弦理论的α'修正中存在Bergshoeff--de Roo（BdR）型平行性——带挠率的Lorentz联络与Yang-Mills联络在结构上平行对应。作者希望在一个统一框架（显式T-对偶）下同时研究这两种联络及其代数结构，并探索杂弦的Chern-Simons结构与反常消除条件的几何起源。

Method: 作者构建了一个同时包含Lorentz扇区和规范扇区的"mega-space"，在该空间中建立世界sheet电流代数。通过推广Poláček-Siegel方案，计算扩展生成元的对易子来确定广义联络。进一步，通过构造"nachos"算符（弦论协变导数）并检查其Jacobi恒等式来推导Green-Schwarz反常消除条件。

Result: （1）该mega-space电流代数将杂弦Chern-Simons结构几何嵌入到广义vielbein和曲率中；（2）Green-Schwarz反常消除条件被证明等价于"nachos"算符代数满足Jacobi恒等式的要求，为反常消除提供了代数层面的几何解释。

Conclusion: 这项工作为杂弦理论中的Lorentz与规范联络提供了统一的T-对偶协变描述，深化了对BdR平行性的理解，并将Green-Schwarz反常消除条件内在化为弦论协变导数代数的Jacobi恒等式要求，暗示反常消除具有深刻的几何与代数根源。

Abstract: We study the Lorentz and gauge connections in heterotic string theories within a framework of manifest T-duality. A central motivation is the Bergshoeff--de Roo (BdR) type parallelism between the torsionful Lorentz and Yang-Mills connections in heterotic $α'$-corrections. We formulate worldsheet current algebras in a mega-space that incorporates the Lorentz and gauge sectors simultaneously. By generalizing the Poláček-Siegel scheme, we evaluate the commutators of the extended generators and determine the generalized connections. We show that this mega-space current algebra geometrically embeds the heterotic Chern--Simons structures into the generalized vielbeins and curvatures. We also show that the Green-Schwarz anomaly cancellation condition follows from the Jacobi identity for the algebra of stringy covariant derivatives, the ``nachos'' operators $\triangleright_{\mathcal{M}}$.

</details>


### [104] [Interior zeros of supersymmetric indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28143)
*Yu Nakayama,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了超对称指标的内部零点（|q|<1）与算术系数δ(ν)指数增长之间的等价关系，并证明了这些零点可从有限个q级数系数检测，阻碍自由或s-约束红外描述，并用4d N=1 SU(2) SQCD示例验证。巨引力子展开表明内部零点是有限N效应，可由单个巨引力子的能量定位，在N=4 U(N) Schur指标中已验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超对称指标（尤其是超级共形指标）的零点结构对于判断理论是否拥有自由或s-约束的红外描述至关重要。此前内部零点的物理含义和检测方法尚不清楚，本文旨在建立内部零点与底层算术系数增长之间的严格联系，并提供从q级数展开中直接检测内部零点的可行性方案。

Method: 本文通过数学分析建立超对称指标的内部零点（|q|<1区域内）与算术系数δ(ν)（超对称zeta函数的基础系数）指数增长率之间的充要关系。关键洞见是每个δ(ν)可由有限个q级数系数决定，因此内部零点的存在性仅从q展开即可判定。同时引入巨引力子展开分析内部零点在有限N时的物理来源，将其归因于单个巨引力子的能量贡献。

Result: 证明了内部零点存在当且仅当算术系数以最近零点决定的速率指数增长；展示了内部零点阻碍自由或s-约束红外描述，并以4d N=1 SU(2) SQCD为例验证；通过巨引力子展开证明内部零点是有限N效应，其位置由单个巨引力子的能量确定，并在N=4 U(N) Schur指标中给出明确验证。

Conclusion: 超对称指标的内部零点具有清晰的算术判定准则（δ(ν)指数增长），是有限N的巨引力子效应，且其存在明确排除了自由或s-约束的红外描述可能性，为判定4d N=1超对称量子场论的红外行为提供了新工具。

Abstract: A supersymmetric index has interior zeros ($|q|<1$) if and only if the arithmetic coefficients $δ(ν)$ underlying the supersymmetric zeta function grow exponentially at a rate set by the nearest zero. Each $δ(ν)$ follows from finitely many $q$-series coefficients, so interior zeros are detectable from the expansion alone and obstruct a free or s-confining infrared description, as we show for 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $SU(2)$ SQCD. With a giant graviton expansion interior zeros are a finite $N$ effect, located by the energy of one giant graviton, verified for the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $U(N)$ Schur index.

</details>


### [105] [Nonlocal four-fermion theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28160)
*F. M. Belchior,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov,P. J. Porfirio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非局域形状因子变形的狄拉克算子所定义的非局域四费米子理论。通过引入辅助标量场推导了平均场有效作用量和能隙方程，并针对指数衰减型和振荡型两种形状因子得到了显式能隙核。利用IR/UV匹配方法，发现双曲型形状因子增强能隙积分并降低临界耦合，而振荡型形状因子则抑制能隙积分并提高临界耦合。论文还通过Matsubara求和与修正的围道表示完成了有限温度和有限密度扩展，并在Λ→∞时恢复局域热能隙方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常的NJL/Gross-Neveu模型是四费米子理论的局域版本，但局域化假设往往不够现实。研究者希望探讨非局域形状因子对四费米子理论的影响，特别是形状因子作为狄拉克算子的矩阵函数这一技术难题，以及其对动态质量生成和临界耦合的物理效应。

Method: 将非局域四费米子理论与矩阵值形状因子耦合，引入辅助标量场后推导平均场有效作用量和能隙方程。由于形状因子是狄拉克算子的矩阵函数，逆传播子需在由恒等算子和p算符生成的闭代数中处理。具体选取f_I=e^{-∂/Λ}（双曲型）和f_II=e^{-i∂/Λ}（振荡型）两种形状因子，得到显式能隙核。采用IR/UV匹配方法，在中间尺度M≪Ω≪Λ处将动量积分分为红外和紫外区域分别解析展开，并与局域NJL/Gross-Neveu结果比较。有限温度和密度情形通过Matsubara求和及修正的围道表示处理。

Result: 双曲型形状因子（指数衰减）增强了能隙积分、降低了临界耦合；振荡型形状因子（指数振荡）抑制了能隙积分、升高了临界耦合。在Λ→∞极限下，有限温度、有限密度的热能隙方程恢复到局域NJL/Gross-Neveu形式。

Conclusion: 非局域形状因子的选择对四费米子理论的动力学对称性破缺有定性影响：衰减型形状因子有利于质量生成，而振荡型形状因子抑制质量生成。形状因子作为矩阵函数处理是必要的，这一框架在有限温度和密度下也保持一致性，并可通过Λ→∞恢复局域极限。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate and analyze a nonlocal four-fermion theory in which the usual Dirac operator is deformed by an entire nonlocal form factor. After introducing an auxiliary scalar field, we derive the mean-field effective action and the corresponding gap equation for the dynamical mass. A central technical point of the analysis is that the nonlocal form factor is a matrix function of the Dirac operator, so the inverse propagator must be treated as an element of the closed algebra generated by the identity and $\not{\!p}$ operators, rather than as a purely scalar quantity. We obtain explicit expressions for the gap kernel for two representative choices of a form factor, $f_{I}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-\not{\partial}/Λ}$ and $f_{II}(\not{\!\partial})=e^{-i\not{\partial}/Λ}$. Following the IR/UV matching method used in the recent Dirac-like nonlocal spinor theory [1], the momentum integral is split at an intermediate scale $M\ll Ω\ll Λ$, expanded analytically in the infrared and ultraviolet regions, and compared with the usual local NJL/Gross-Neveu result. We show that the hyperbolic form factor enhances the gap integral and lowers the critical coupling, whereas the oscillatory form factor suppresses it and raises the critical coupling. The finite-temperature and finite-density extension is formulated through Matsubara sums and a corrected contour representation, with the local thermal gap equation recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [106] [Light-Front approach to $4d$ massless Higher-Spin interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28183)
*Mattia Serrani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在光前（Light-Front）框架下研究四维无质量高自旋场论的相互作用，通过分析庞加莱代数在四次阶的闭合性，系统性构造并分类了局域高自旋理论，揭示了其与自对偶杨-米尔斯理论、引力及Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity的联系，并进一步厘清了天球CFT中OPE结合性、树级振幅消失、雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自旋相互作用理论长期以来是场论中的重要难题，尤其是自旋大于2的无质量场的自洽耦合受到多种约束的限制。作者希望系统性地理解四维无质量高自旋相互作用的可能性和其代数结构，从而为高自旋引力、天球全息等方向提供新的理论基础。

Method: 采用光前（Light-Front）量子化框架，在四次阶求解庞加莱代数的闭合条件。具体包括：(1) 求解平直空间中的光锥四次全纯约束，系统分类所有单导数和双导数理论；(2) 分析非全纯约束（涉及MHV与anti-MHV顶点）以恢复杨-米尔斯和引力，并证明多引力子互作用的不自洽性；(3) 在包含更高导数三次顶点的情形下求解完整四次约束，利用旋量-螺旋度形式加上局域性（一致因子化）分类所有幺正局域高自旋理论并确定四次振幅。

Result: (1) 发现平直空间中存在无穷多个局域高自旋互作用理论，可分为有限场或无限场两类；(2) 所有单导数和双导数理论被完整分类，对应于规范与引力相互作用的高自旋推广，它们是自对偶杨-米尔斯和引力的高自旋扩展的一致子扇区（也是Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity的截断）；(3) 阐明了天球CFT中OPE结合性、一般运动学下树级振幅消失、动力学代数的雅可比恒等式与光锥全纯约束之间的等价关系；(4) 证实在MHV与anti-MHV顶点同时存在时，仅杨-米尔斯和引力自洽，多引力子互作用不自洽；(5) 一旦加入更高导数三次顶点，存在四次约束的非平凡解，并分类出所有幺正局域高自旋理论，发现新的准手征局域理论族，且确定了所有局域高自旋四次振幅。

Conclusion: 该研究为四维无质量高自旋相互作用的局域理论提供了系统的构造与完整分类框架，统一了光锥约束、天球全息OPE结合性与动力学代数结构等不同视角，揭示了一类新的准手征高自旋理论，深化了对高自旋相互作用可能性的理解。

Abstract: This thesis studies $4d$ massless higher-spin interactions in the Light-Front approach by analysing the closure of the Poincaré algebra at quartic order. We first solve the light-cone quartic holomorphic constraint in flat space and show the existence of infinitely many interacting local higher-spin theories with either a finite or infinite number of fields. We classify all one- and two-derivative theories, corresponding to higher-spin extensions of gauge and gravitational interactions. These are consistent subsectors of higher-spin extensions of self-dual Yang--Mills and gravity, themselves truncations of Chiral Higher-Spin Gravity. We then clarify the relation between the OPE associativity in celestial CFT, the vanishing of tree-level amplitudes for generic kinematics, the Jacobi identity of the associated ''gauge algebra'' (kinematical algebra), and the light-cone holomorphic constraints. Finally, we investigate the non-holomorphic quartic constraint involving both MHV and anti-MHV vertices. We recover the existence of Yang-Mills theory and gravity, and the inconsistency of interacting multi-graviton theories. We then show that once higher-derivative cubic vertices are included, nontrivial solutions to the full quartic constraint exist. We classify all unitary local higher-spin theories, identify new families of local quasi-chiral theories, and determine all local higher-spin four-point amplitudes using the spinor-helicity formalism together with locality in the form of consistent factorisation.

</details>


### [107] [Missing Descendants in the Carrollian Conformal Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28400)
*Yu-fan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了完整的Carrollian共形表示，通过将K^0作用生成的独立descendant链与其所有平移后代包含在内。在2D和3D中推导了对应的局域算符、轨结构并检验了Casimir，利用全局两点Ward恒等式确定了运动学因子和选择规则。与普通共形对称性不同，Carrollian共形对称性通常只能将关联函数确定为Carrollian不变量的任意函数而非常数。完整表示中包含C_2>0的扇区，可能描述平面时空全息中的有质量态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Carrollian共形代数中[P^0,K^0]=0导致由时间平移P^0生成的后代算子会被K^0湮灭，因此标准共形族不包含由K^0逐级降回原算子的独立后代链。为了获得完整的Carrollian共形表示，必须将这缺失的后代链及其所有平移后代纳入考虑。

Method: 构造完整的Carrollian共形表示：同时包含由K^0逐级降低的后代链及其所有平移后代。在2D和3D中推导局域算符和轨结构，并检验Casimir不变量。通过全局两点Ward恒等式固定运动学因子和选择规则，分别处理磁性非接触分支和电性接触分支。

Result: 得到了完整的Carrollian共形表示及其对应的局域算符和轨结构。两点Ward恒等式确定了各轨构型的运动学因子和选择规则。完整表示中包含了C_2=m^2>0的扇区（2D中为κρ-β^2>0，3D中为κρ-β→^2>0），这些扇区可能对应于平面全息中的有质量态。

Conclusion: Carrollian共形对称性对关联函数的约束比普通共形对称性弱，只能将关联函数确定到Carrollian不变量的任意函数级别。完整表示中包含正Casimir扇区，为平面时空全息中有质量态的描提供了可能。

Abstract: In the Carrollian conformal algebra, the relation $[P^0,K^0]=0$ implies that $K^0$ annihilates all operators generated from a primary by temporal translation $P^0$. The standard conformal family defined by translation descendants does not contain the independent descendant chain that $K^0$ lowers successively to the primary. We construct the complete Carrollian conformal representation by including this chain together with all of its translation descendants. We derived the corresponding local operators, orbit structures, and checked their Casimirs in 2D and 3D. For each orbit configuration, the global two-point Ward identities fix the kinematic factors and selection rules for both magnetic non-contact branches and electric contact branches. Unlike ordinary conformal symmetry, Carrollian conformal symmetry generally determines the correlators only up to arbitrary functions of Carrollian invariants rather than constants. The complete representation contains sectors with $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-β^2>0$ for 2D and $\mathcal{C}_2=m^2=κρ-\vecβ^{\,2}>0$ for 3D, which may describe massive states in flat holography.

</details>


### [108] [Holography in the linearized quantum gravity regime and modular crossed product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27337)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS/CFT半经典框架下，利用全息映射（保距嵌入映射）严格证明了JLMS条件，并通过模交叉积构造，在dressed Type-II代数框架下严格证明了真空减除的HRT公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS/CFT对应中，如何严格从全息映射出发推导量子信息理论中的关键结果（如相对熵的等价性JLMS条件，以及纠缠熵的HRT公式）是重要课题。本文旨在在更严谨的数学框架下证明这些已知物理结果。

Method: 论文采用半经典AdS/CFT框架，将体场论线性化为纯AdS背景上的线性化Einstein方程。利用体GNS Hilbert空间到边界CFT GNS Hilbert空间的保距全息映射，假设该映射保持真空并允许AdS-Rindler模重建。基于此推导JLMS条件；对于因果楔中的局域半经典相干激发，借助模交叉积构造在dressed Type-II代数中推导HRT公式。

Result: 在code subspace中，体因果楔代数的相对熵与边界CFT观测者代数中的相对熵严格匹配（JLMS条件）；对于局域半经典相干激发，dressed Type-II代数中双对偶CFT态熵的态依赖部分满足真空减除的HRT公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为AdS/CFT中相对熵的体-边界等价性提供了从全息映射出发的严格推导，并将HRT公式嵌入到模交叉积构造的严格数学框架中，深化了对量子引力中熵概念的理解。

Abstract: Within the semi-classical regime of AdS/CFT correspondence, we consider the limit where the bulk dynamical field is linearized metric perturbations satisfying linearized Einstein equations over background pure AdS spacetime. AdS/CFT correspondence gives us a holographic map, which is an isometric embedding map of the GNS Hilbert space of linearized gravity in the bulk (w.r.t. the AdS-invariant vacuum) to the GNS Hilbert space of CFT in the boundary (w.r.t. the Minkowski-invariant vacuum). We assume that the map takes AdS-vacuum in the bulk to CFT-vacuum in the boundary and that it allows AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. Then using this map, we show that for a given ball-shaped region in the boundary $A$, the relative entropy of a bulk state w.r.t. the AdS vacuum in the algebra of causal wedge associated to $A$ matches with the relative entropy of the dual CFT state w.r.t. the CFT vacuum in the algebra of CFT observables in $A$ in the code subspace, which is known as Jafferis-Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Suh (JLMS) condition. Furthermore, for localized semi-classical coherent excitations in the causal wedge associated to $A$ which corresponds to perturbed bulk geometry, we show rigorously using modular crossed product construction that the state-dependent part of entropy of the dual CFT state in the dressed Type-II algebra associated to $A$ satisfies vacuum subtracted Hubeney-Rangamani-Takayanagi (HRT) formula.

</details>


### [109] [Covariant variation and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28420)
*Wen-Bin Liu,Jiang Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义了张量场的协变变分算子，通过结合李导数与度规变分得到，该算子保持度规、缩并和霍奇对偶，但其对易子因反常而不封闭。论文推导了其代数与几何性质，并与Kosmann导数进行了比较，还引入了扩展算子族，并应用该算子及其反常到电磁螺旋度通量和p-形式等物理场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微分几何中的李导数虽然保持张量结构，但并不能保持度规；而协变导数虽然与度规相容，却不与李括号自然相容。作者希望构造一种既保持度规、缩并和霍奇对偶等关键结构，又具有明确几何意义的变分算子，以弥合李导数与度规变分之间的鸿沟，并探索其反常（anomaly）带来的物理后果。

Method: 作者将张量场的李导数与度规变分相结合，定义了一种协变变分算子。他们系统地推导了该算子的代数性质（如与缩并、霍奇对偶的相容性）和几何性质，分析其对易子的反常闭合性；将其与Kosmann导数对比，再将两者结合得到同时具有时空与Lorentz结构的总协变变分。进一步引入包含仿射联络、李导数和协变变分在内的扩展算子族，并具体应用于超旋转（superrotation）下反常所导致的电磁螺旋度通量，以及d=2p+2维中的p-形式。

Result: 证明协变变分算子保持度规、缩并和霍奇对偶，但其李括号对易子存在反常。通过与Kosmann导数结合，构造出总协变变分算子，适用于同时具有时空和Lorentz结构的场，且所有这些算子均属于度量李导数范畴。在四维零超曲面上，协变变分的反常沿超旋转产生了电磁螺旋度通量；在任意时空维数下，特别是d=2p+2维中的p-形式，也得到了相应应用。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的张量变分框架，即协变变分算子，在保持关键几何结构的同时揭示了其反常性质，并展示了该反常在电磁螺旋度通量和更高维p-形式理论中的物理意义。这一框架有可能为理解引力、规范场与守恒流之间的关系提供新的工具。

Abstract: We define a covariant variation of tensor fields by combining its Lie derivative with the metric variation. This operator preserves the metric, contractions, and Hodge duality, but its commutator is not closed due to an anomaly. We derive its algebraic and geometric properties, and compare it with the Kosmann derivative. Combining the covariant variation with Kosmann derivative gives total covariant variation for the fields with both spacetime and Lorentz structure, all of which belong to the metric Lie derivative. Moreover, we introduce families of extended operators which contain the affine connection, Lie derivative, and covariant variation. From the anomaly of the covariant variation along the superrotation, an electromagnetic helicity flux appears at null hypersurfaces in four dimensions. We also apply the covariant variation and its anomaly to tensor fields in arbitrary spacetime dimensions, and especially focus on the $p$-forms in $d=2p+2$ dimensions.

</details>


### [110] [Hayden--Preskill recovery at finite temperature on a quantum processor: dynamics and initial state from the SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28486)
*Jeongho Bang,Moongul Byun,Kyoungho Cho,Keun-Young Kim,Hyeonsoo Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Hayden-Preskill协议基础上做了两个扩展：通过SWAP门将后注入扰序器与初始态关联起来，并考虑有限温度下的恢复。研究表明，在修正协议中信息能成功恢复（后选择概率不可忽略且条件保真度高），两者均与温度成正比。作者还推导了均匀算子扩展假设下的晚期时间解析估计，并在IBM超导处理器上使用N=8马约拉纳的二元稀疏SYK哈密顿量进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Hayden-Preskill恢复协议中，后注入扰序器与初始态之间没有关联，这限制了协议的应用场景。作者希望扩展该设置，使其更贴近实际物理情形，并探索有限温度下的信息恢复行为。

Method: 1) 修改协议：引入SWAP门使扰序器与初始态相关联，并考虑有限温度初始态；2) 分别分析后选择概率和条件保真度随温度的变化；3) 在均匀算子扩展假设下推导晚期时间解析估计；4) 在IBM超导处理器上使用N=8马约拉纳的二元稀疏SYK哈密顿量进行量子计算实验实现，并采用基于SWAP的纠错降噪方案。

Result: 信息在修正协议下能成功恢复：后选择概率不可忽略且条件保真度较高。两者均与温度成正比，反映低温下初始态纠缠减少。解析估计与数值结果吻合良好，表明强扰序对成功恢复至关重要。量子硬件实验保留了定性恢复动力学特征，且SWAP纠错方案改善了后选择概率和条件保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Hayden-Preskill协议，证明了即使扰序器与初始态相关联（而非完全独立）以及处于有限温度时，信息仍可通过强扰序过程成功恢复。研究揭示了温度与信息恢复质量的正相关关系，并验证了协议在真实量子硬件上的可行性，同时展示了SWAP纠错方案的实际价值。

Abstract: In the original Hayden--Preskill recovery, the post-injection scrambler and initial state are {\it not related}. We extend this setup in two ways: by using a SWAP gate so that the scrambler and initial state are {\it related}, and by considering recovery at {\it finite} temperature. For this modified protocol, we show that the information is successfully recovered in the sense that the postselection probability is non-negligible and the conditional fidelity is large. We find that both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity are proportional to temperature, reflecting the reduced entanglement of the initial state at lower temperatures. We also derive their late-time analytic estimates under the assumption of uniform operator spreading and show that they agree well with the numerical results. This demonstrates that strong scrambling is important for successful information recovery. Implementing the protocol on an IBM superconducting processor using a binary sparse SYK Hamiltonian with $N = 8$ Majoranas, we observe that the data retain the qualitative recovery dynamics and that a SWAP-based error-mitigation scheme improves both the postselection probability and the conditional fidelity.

</details>


### [111] [Soft charges and zero modes at null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28543)
*Dušan Đorđ ević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论零无穷远边界上的全局零模歧义如何修改场论在零边界处的正则结构，并展示其产生一种新的准局域边缘可观测量，对荷代数产生影响。该零模可出现在任何零边界（如零无穷远和黑洞视界）上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零边界的渐近对称性为理解场论的软扇区提供了强大窗口，揭示了超越标准体动力学的守恒荷和无限维对称代数。虽然这些结构通常与残余规范变换相关，但零边界还表现出一种特征性的全局零模歧义，其物理后果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 本文讨论零模如何修改场论在零边界处的正则结构，并证明该零模会产生一种新的准局域边缘可观测量，进而对荷代数产生影响。

Result: 零模在零边界处催生了一种新的准局域边缘可观测量，该可观测量对荷代数具有影响，且可出现在任何类型的零边界上——包括零无穷远和有限距离边界（如黑洞视界）。

Conclusion: 零模歧义并非仅是形式上的不规范性问题，而是具有实际物理后果的全局特征；它为理解零边界上的荷代数和边缘可观测量提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Asymptotic symmetries at null boundaries provide a powerful window into the soft sector of field theories, revealing conserved charges and infinite-dimensional symmetry algebras beyond the standard bulk dynamics. While these structures are usually associated with residual gauge transformations, null boundaries also exhibit a characteristic global zero-mode ambiguity, whose physical consequences are less explored. We discuss how this zero mode modifies the canonical structure of field theories at null boundaries. In particular, we show that it gives rise to a new quasilocal edge observable, with consequences to the charge algebra. It can arise on any kind of null boundaries, both at the null infinity and the finite distance such as a black hole horizon.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Trace Seiberg Dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28628)
*Jonathan J. Heckman,Shani Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法（Transformer和多层感知机）来高效判定超对称quiver规范理论中的Seiberg对偶性，其数学本质是判断quiver的突变（mutation）等价性。研究发现，对于约10个节点的quiver，神经网络架构优于确定性算法，而辅以路径查找算法（"quiver版Google Maps"）可进一步提升搜索效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶性在物理系统中至关重要，但从计算角度出发，判定两个系统是否对偶往往非常困难。即使在规则完全已知的情况下，如何高效建立两个系统的对偶关系仍是一个开放问题。本文旨在用机器学习方法解决超对称quiver规范理论中Seiberg对偶的判定问题。

Method: 将Seiberg对偶的判定数学化为quiver的突变等价性判定（类"learning to unknot"问题）。采用Transformer和多层感知机等不同网络架构进行训练，并与确定性算法进行对比；同时结合已有的路径查找算法作为辅助，形成混合搜索策略来提高效率与准确率。

Result: 对于约10个节点的quiver，Transformer和多层感知机等网络架构在判定Seiberg对偶方面优于确定性算法；在此基础上补充路径查找算法（相当于"quiver的Google Maps"）后，搜索策略的效率和准确性得到进一步提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用机器学习建立不同对偶性提供了实用工具，同时也揭示了不同网络架构学习Seiberg对偶追踪的能力差异。作者预期这类问题可作为前沿AI模型在理论物理中应用的基准测试。

Abstract: Dualities play an important role in establishing both microscopic and emergent phenomena in a wide range of physical systems. In practice, though, it can often be computationally challenging to establish when two systems are dual, even when all of the "rules of the game" are well-known. Said differently, when confronted with two systems, how can one efficiently establish that they are in fact dual? In this paper we use machine learning methods to address this question for Seiberg dualities of supersymmetric quiver gauge theories. Mathematically, this involves establishing mutations of quivers, which is in turn a variation on the theme of "learning to unknot". On the one hand, this leads us to a practical tool for establishing the computational complexity of different dualities. On the other hand, it also allows us to study how different network architectures learn how to trace Seiberg dualities. We find that for quivers with a modest number of quiver nodes (of order $10$), different network architectures consisting of transformers and multi-layer perceptrons tend to outperform deterministic algorithms. Supplementing the network by well-established pathfinder algorithms (essentially "Google Maps for quivers") leads to an additional improvement in the efficiency and accuracy of the search strategy. We anticipate that this class of questions can serve as a useful benchmark for frontier AI models applied to theoretical physics.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Clifford-Appell formulation of a Dirac-type Kronig-Penney model in condensed matter physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27920)
*L. Aceto,P. A. Grassi,H. Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了(N+1)维Clifford分析中广义Kronig-Penney模型对应的稳态Dirac方程。通过引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统重构为耦合的一阶系统，利用特征超复变量将其解耦为广义Helmholtz方程，并借助二元Clifford-Appell多项式基构造显式级数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广义Kronig-Penney模型提供统一的Clifford解析框架，建立广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统之间的显式联系，从而在高维超复函数论中系统求解稳态Dirac方程。

Method: (1) 引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统重构为两个Clifford值场的耦合一阶系统；(2) 通过特征超复变量将系统解耦为两个广义Helmholtz方程；(3) 引入适应广义Cauchy-Riemann算子的二元Clifford-Appell多项式基；(4) 利用Appell展开将微分问题转化为展开系数的代数递推关系，得到解的显式级数表示。

Result: 得到了稳态Dirac方程解的显式级数表示，建立了一个统一的Clifford解析自由传播公式，并阐明了广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统之间明确的联系。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维超复分析中的Dirac方程提供了系统化的求解方法，将微分方程问题转化为代数递推问题，统一了广义Helmholtz方程与Clifford-Appell多项式理论，为后续处理Kronig-Penney模型的带结构分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

</details>


### [114] [The reduced Dirac structure of General Relativity on manifolds with corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28262)
*Alberto S. Cattaneo,Filippo Fila-Robattino,Manuel Tecchiolli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了四维Palatini-Cartan引力的角点Poisson结构。通过流形带边界上经典引力描述的边界约束代数，在角点场空间上构建了pre-Dirac结构，并通过约化过程得到极大Dirac结构（即Poisson双向量场的图）。该Poisson结构可等价地描述为仿射Poisson结构，将约化角点理论自然地展现为BF型理论，并引出BF²V表述，为Palatini-Cartan引力的体、边界与角点结构提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力理论中，理解角点（corner）处的几何与代数结构对于研究引力的量子化、纠缠熵以及全息原理具有重要意义。Palatini-Cartan引力作为带挠率引力的一种规范理论表述，其角落结构尚未被系统阐明。作者旨在揭示四维Palatini-Cartan引力在边界约束代数基础上诱导出的角点Poisson结构，从而为体/边界/角点结构提供统一描述框架。

Method: 作者从流形带边界的经典描述出发，利用边界约束代数构造角点场空间上的pre-Dirac结构；随后通过约化程序得到极大Dirac结构，并将其识别为Poisson双向量场的图。进一步证明该Poisson结构可等价地重写为仿射（affine）Poisson结构，从而将约化后的角点理论重新表述为BF型理论，并据此导出BF²V公式化。

Result: 得到了四维Palatini-Cartan引力角点处的Poisson结构（通过pre-Dirac结构与约化过程获得，最终识别为Poisson双向量场的图）；证明了该结构具有等价的仿射Poisson描述，并将角点理论重新表述为BF-型理论（BF²V formulation）。

Conclusion: 该工作为Palatini-Cartan引力提供了包含体、边界和角点在内的统一框架（即BF²V表述），为后续研究引力的角点对称性、代数量子化等相关问题奠定了几何基础。

Abstract: In this paper, the corner Poisson structure of four-dimensional Palatini-Cartan gravity is derived. Building on the classical description of gravity on manifolds with boundary, specifically on the boundary constraint algebra, a pre- Dirac structure on the space of corner fields is obtained together with a reduction procedure that yields a maximal Dirac structure, identified as the graph of a Poisson bivector field, on the reduced space of corner fields. It is further shown that this Poisson structure admits an equivalent affine Poisson description, which naturally exhibits the reduced corner theory as a $BF$-like theory and leads to a BF$^2$V formulation. This provides the basis for a unified framework for the bulk, boundary, and corner structures of Palatini-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [115] [Consistent symmetry breaking and topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28181)
*Yuezhao Li,Guo Chuan Thiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了等变指标在对称性破缺下的规范弱/强二分法，并研究了其与粗几何（宏观）指标的关系。核心原理是：在对称群下不变的算子在其任何有限指数子群下也不变，因此等变指标在对称性破缺时必须保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从对称性群作用的角度，利用对称性破缺必须保持等变指标一致性的基本原则，建立等变指标的规范弱/强二分法，并建立其与粗几何或宏观指标之间的联系。这一思想源于凝聚态物理中拓扑绝缘体的理论。

Method: 本文方法基于以下核心观察：若算子在某个对称群下不变，则它也在该群的任何有限指数子群下不变。因此，算子的等变指标在不同有限指数子群下必须一致（即满足对称性破缺一致性）。基于此原理，作者建立等变指标的规范弱/强二分法，并进一步探讨其与粗几何（宏观）指标之间的关联。

Result: 建立了等变指标的规范弱/强二分法框架，该框架源于拓扑绝缘体理论中的思想，并确立了与粗几何（宏观）指标之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 等变指标在对称性破缺下具有天然的一致性约束，由此导出的弱/强二分法为理解对称性破缺系统中的拓扑不变量提供了统一框架，并可通过粗几何指标与宏观几何相联系。

Abstract: Operators invariant under a symmetry group are also invariant under any finite-index subgroup. The equivariant indices of such operators must be consistent under symmetry breaking. We use this principle to establish a canonical weak/strong dichotomy for equivariant indices, building on an idea originating in the theory of topological insulators in solid-state physics. We also study the relationship to coarse-geometric, or macroscopic, indices.

</details>


### [116] [Integrability in Asymptotic Symmetries of Spacetime: the $\text{BMS}_3$ scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28454)
*Corentin Vitel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Fuentealba等人(2018)提出的$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层次结构的证明，采用不同的结构方法论。作者从多项式符号环上的变分复形出发，构造了$\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构及其Nijenhuis算子，并类比讨论了$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形，验证了后者的平直极限可恢复渐近平直情形。此外还给出了$\tau-$方案描述，并通过Lie-Poisson描述表明该$\mathfrak{bms}_3$层次并非唯一。对于一类能量依赖的Schrödinger算子子类，证明了它们的Lax流可由$\mathfrak{bms}_3$的余伴随轨道描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视并完善$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层次结构的证明，采用更系统的结构方法论（如变分复形、双哈密顿结构等），同时澄清该层次的唯一性问题，并建立与能量依赖Schrödinger算子Lax流的联系。

Method: 利用多项式符号环上的变分复形构造$\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构并附加Nijenhuis算子；对$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形做类似论证并检验平直极限；引入$\tau-$方案描述；通过Lie-Poisson描述探讨层次唯一性；分析能量依赖Schrödinger算子的Lax流与$\mathfrak{bms}_3$余伴随轨道的关系。

Result: 成功在变分复形框架下构造了$\mathfrak{bms}_3$双哈密顿结构及Nijenhuis算子；$\mathrm{AdS}_3$情形的平直极限确实恢复渐近平直情形；Lie-Poisson分析表明该$\mathfrak{bms}_3$层次不具唯一性；证明特定能量依赖Schrödinger算子的Lax流可由$\mathfrak{bms}_3$余伴随轨道描述。

Conclusion: 本文从多种结构视角（变分复形、双哈密顿结构、$\tau-$方案、Lie-Poisson）对$\mathfrak{bms}_3$可积层次进行了系统重构和深化，确认了其与非唯一性及能量依赖Schrödinger算子Lax流之间的深层联系，为该可积体系提供了更完备的数学基础。

Abstract: We revisit the proof of a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$integrable hierarchy introduced in Fuentealba et al. (JHEP, 2018), using different structural methodologies. Specifically, from the variational complex on the ring of polynomial symbols, a $\mathfrak{bms}_3$ bi-Hamiltonian structure is constructed on which a Nijenhuis operator can be attached. A similar argument is made for the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ case, where we check that the flat limit of the latter recovers the asymptotically flat situation. An alternative $τ-$scheme description is also presented. Moreover, a Lie-Poisson description suggests that such a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$hierarchy is not unique. For a subclass of so-called energy-dependent Schrödinger operators, it is shown that their Lax flows are described by the coadjoint orbits of $\mathfrak{bms}_3$.

</details>


### [117] [Redundant moments of non-melonic random tensor models: simplifying the tensor bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28518)
*Carlos I. Perez-Sanchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意张量模型中，大N极限下的melonic族矩（moments）是冗余的，即任何melonic算子C的大N期望值仅取决于其degree（度），而不依赖于其他细节。这一结果将张量bootstrap方法中构建正半定矩阵所需的核心独立分量数量大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量bootstrap方法（tensor bootstrap）是格点规范理论和随机矩阵bootstrap中正半定Toeplitz和Hankel矩阵的推广，但其核心正半定矩阵包含大量独立分量，计算成本高昂。如果能够证明某些矩（melonic矩）在大N极限下是冗余的，就能显著减少bootstrap方法中需要处理的独立变量的数量，提高方法的可行性和效率。

Method: 作者在任意张量模型的框架下，严格证明melonic族的大N矩具有冗余性：任何melonic算子C在大N极限下的期望值仅通过其degree（度）来决定。同时证明了对melonic矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程也满足类似的结论。该方法的关键在于证明melonic算子的期望值对C的具体结构不敏感，只依赖于其degree。

Result: 论文证明了两点核心结果：(1) 任意melonic算子C在大N极限下的期望值只取决于C的degree；(2) melonic矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程具有类似的性质。该结果的适用范围不仅限于melonic张量模型本身，具有更广泛的普适性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了张量bootstrap核心矩阵中大量melonic矩条目在大N极限下是冗余的，大幅减少了张量bootstrap方法中需要追踪的独立矩阵分量数目。这一结果适用于任意张量模型，不仅限于melonic张量模型，为张量bootstrap方法的实际应用和计算效率提升提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove the redundancy of a family (the so-called melonic family) of large-$N$ moments in arbitrary tensor models. This reduces the number of independent entries in the positive semidefinite matrices that build the core of the tensor bootstrap (for tensor models there are already several generalizations of the positive semidefinite Toeplitz and Hankel matrices of lattice gauge theory and random matrix bootstrap, respectively). More concretely, we prove that any melonic operator $C$ at large-$N$ has an expectation value that depends on $C$ exclusively through its degree. A similar statement is shown here to hold for the Schwinger-Dyson equations of melonic moments. The strength of our result is also its scope, which is not limited to melonic tensor models.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [The toric CR Yamabe problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27441)
*Eveline Legendre,Carlo Scarpa,Christina W. Tønnesen-Friedman*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定点自由环面作用下的等变CR Yamabe问题，在环面接触流形情形下将其转化为凸多面体域上一对函数所满足的椭圆PDE边值问题，并证明许多接触环面流形可配备具有不同CR Yamabe不变量符号的相容CR结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变CR Yamabe问题是CR几何中的核心问题之一，其研究动机源于Sasakian流形上的常数量曲率问题。环面情形下该问题可以简化为更易处理的PDE边值问题，从而为构造具有不同CR Yamabe不变量符号的例子提供途径。

Method: 作者将无固定点的环面作用下的CR Yamabe问题与一个凸多面体域上的双函数椭圆PDE边值问题建立等价关系，利用环面对称性将原问题降维，并通过构造具体解来证明CR Yamabe不变量符号的多样性。

Result: 文章提供了该边值问题的具体解的例子，并证明了大量接触环面流形上存在相容CR结构，使得对应的CR Yamabe不变量具有不同的符号（正与负）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了等变CR Yamabe问题与凸域上椭圆PDE边值问题之间的等价性，丰富了CR Yamabe不变量符号分类的结果，为Sasakian几何中常数量曲率结构的存在性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study the equivariant CR Yamabe problem for a fixed-point-free torus action on a co-oriented compact contact manifold. Such examples arise naturally in the study of Sasakian manifolds with constant scalar curvature. In the toric case, we show that this problem is equivalent to a kind of boundary value problem for an elliptic PDE on a pair of functions defined on a convex, polyhedral domain. We provide examples of solutions and prove that many contact toric manifolds admit compatible CR structures with distinct signs of CR Yamabe invariants.

</details>


### [119] [Minimal hypersurfaces of Morse index one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27444)
*Otis Chodosh,Matilde Gianocca*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在$\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$中，具有有限全曲率和莫尔斯指标为1的完备、连通、嵌入式极小超曲面必为高维悬链面（catenoid）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在刻画具有莫尔斯指标为1的完整极小超曲面的分类问题，推广经典的二维结果至高维情形。

Method: 采用了几何分析的方法，利用极小超曲面的稳定性理论、莫尔斯指标的相关性质以及有限全曲率的约束条件进行证明。

Result: 得到了一个严格的分类定理：在上述假设条件下，该极小超曲面必然是更高维的悬链面。

Conclusion: 该结果完善了极小超曲面的拓扑与几何分类理论，将低维已知结论推广至任意维数，为极小曲面理论提供了重要的结构定理。

Abstract: We prove that a complete, connected, embedded, minimal hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ with finite total curvature and Morse index one is the higher-dimensional catenoid.

</details>


### [120] [Existence and Regularity of Extremal Metrics for the Conformal Dirichlet-to-Robin Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27489)
*Samuel Pérez-Ayala*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了维数至少为3的带边界紧致流形上Dirichlet-to-Robin映射的特征值变分性质。证明了Type II Yamabe度量极值化第一归一化特征值泛函并刻画了所有极值点；当共形类中该算子至少有2个负特征值时，证明了存在广义度量极大化第二归一化特征值，且每个这样的度量要么定义了边界上变号的Escobar-Yamabe型方程的解，要么是到单位欧氏球的弱自由边界调和映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirichlet-to-Robin映射的特征值变分问题，该问题与流形上的Yamabe型问题、共形几何及调和映射理论密切相关，是几何分析中特征值优化与变分方法的交叉前沿问题。

Method: 利用变分方法和几何分析工具，构造广义度量空间框架，结合Type II Yamabe度量理论以及Escobar-Yamabe方程和自由边界调和映射的变分技术，对归一化特征值泛函进行极值性分析。

Result: (1) 证明Type II Yamabe度量极值化第一归一化特征值泛函，并完整刻画了所有极值点；(2) 在至少两个负特征值的条件下，证明存在广义度量极大化第二归一化特征值；(3) 揭示了极大化度量与Escobar-Yamabe型方程（边界变号解）或弱自由边界调和映射之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Dirichlet-to-Robin映射特征值变分问题与Yamabe型几何方程和自由边界调和映射之间的深刻联系，为高维带边界流形上的特征值优化问题提供了完整的变分刻画。

Abstract: We study the variational properties of the spectrum of the Dirichlet-to-Robin map $\mathcal{D}_g$ on connected compact manifolds with boundary of dimension at least three. For the first eigenvalue, we show that Type II Yamabe metrics extremize the first normalized eigenvalue functional, and we characterize all extremals. If $[g]$ is a conformal class for which $\mathcal{D}_g$ has at least two negative eigenvalues, then we show the existence of a generalized metric that maximizes the second normalized eigenvalue of $\mathcal{D}_g$ in the conformal class. Moreover, we show that each such metric either defines a solution to an Escobar--Yamabe type equation on manifolds with boundary that changes sign along the boundary, or a weakly free-boundary harmonic map into the unit Euclidean ball.

</details>


### [121] [Volume Stability for Hyperbolic Manifolds and Applications to General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27666)
*Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭合双曲三维流形的尖锐体积稳定性定理：若光滑度量g_i满足数量曲率≥-6且体积收敛到双曲体积，则（子列意义下）度量在去掉体积趋于零的集合后，通过微分同胚在C^0模下逐点收敛到双曲度量。作为应用，建立了Fischer–Moncrief约化哈密顿量在Lorentz锥基态处的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在闭合双曲三维流形上，尖锐双曲体积界（R(g)≥-6隐含Vol≥Vol_h）的等式情形何时被逼近？作者希望建立"近等号"情形下度量的具体收敛模式，即体积收敛是否强制张量性的C^0收敛，从而为Fischer–Moncrief的渐近图景提供严格的体积支配表述。

Method: 证明采用尖锐体积稳定性定理的框架：对满足R(g_i)≥-6且体积趋于双曲体积的度量序列，构造子列、小体积集合Z_i（度量体积趋于零）及大体积光滑域K_i（双曲余体积趋于零），在其上通过微分同胚ψ_i实现ψ_i^*g_i在C^0意义下收敛到双曲度量h。随后将此几何张量级收敛结果应用于CMC归一化后的真空紧致数据，建立Fischer–Moncrief约化哈密顿量的稳定性。

Result: 得到了闭合双曲三维流形上的尖锐体积稳定性定理：近等号情形下度量在去掉消没体积集合后张量性C^0收敛到双曲度量。并由此建立了Fischer–Moncrief约化哈密顿量在Lorentz锥基态处的稳定性，给出其渐近图景的严格体积支配表述。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了尖锐双曲体积不等式近等号情形的刚性/稳定性刻画：体积逼近即强制张量C^0收敛（模去消没体积区域），并对广义相对论中Fischer–Moncrief哈密顿动力学的渐近行为给出了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We prove a sharp volume-stability theorem for closed hyperbolic three-manifolds. Let \((M,h)\) be closed hyperbolic with \(\operatorname{Ric}_h=-2h\), and let \(g_i\) be smooth metrics on \(M\) satisfying $R(g_i)\geq -6, \operatorname{Vol}_{g_i}(M)\longrightarrow \operatorname{Vol}_h(M)$. After passing to a subsequence, there exist \(Z_i\subset M\), smooth domains \(K_i\subset M\), and diffeomorphisms $ψ_i:K_i\longrightarrow M\setminus Z_i $
such that $\operatorname{Vol}_{g_i}(Z_i)\longrightarrow0, \operatorname{Vol}_h(M\setminus K_i)\longrightarrow0, $
and $
\|ψ_i^*g_i-h\|_{C^0(K_i,h)}\longrightarrow0. $
Thus near-equality in the sharp hyperbolic volume bound forces tensorial \(C^0\)-convergence to the hyperbolic metric outside regions of vanishing volume. As an application, we establish stability of the Fischer--Moncrief reduced Hamiltonian at the Lorentz-cone ground state: after CMC normalization, near-minimizing compact vacuum data in the hyperbolic topological class converge, modulo sets of vanishing volume, to the hyperbolic Lorentz-cone geometry in tensorial \(C^0\). This provides a rigorous volume-dominance formulation of the Fischer--Moncrief asymptotic picture.

</details>


### [122] [Contraction Maps Generated by Inverse Mean Curvature Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27711)
*Bang-Xian Han,Zhuo-Nan Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用逆平均曲率流构造保面积归一化的Lipschitz收缩映射，类似于Kim和E. Milman的热流构造方法。该方法在二维情形证明了E. Milman关于黎曼二维球面的收缩猜想，并对边界测地曲率为一的非负曲率圆盘给出了内在结果，还恢复并推广了Lin、Wang和Xu的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构造从圆球面到闭严格凸超曲面、以及从平圆盘到欧氏球中严格凸自由边界超曲面的保面积归一化Lipschitz收缩映射，从而在黎曼流形上建立谱比较和等周比较等几何分析中的关键工具，并证明相关的收缩猜想。

Method: 采用逆平均曲率流（inverse mean curvature flow）来构造这些收缩映射，其思路与Kim和E. Milman用热流构造映射的方法类似，在本质上是将几何流作用于超曲面以生成面积归一化的收缩映射。

Result: 在二维情形下，证明了E. Milman关于二维黎曼球面的收缩猜想，并证明了非负曲率、边界测地曲率为一的圆盘的内在类似结果。同时，这些映射在实际Lipschitz阈值处给出了双边谱比较和映射级别的刚性，恢复了Lin、Wang和Xu的定理。

Conclusion: 逆平均曲率流是一种有效的几何工具，可用于构造保面积归一化的Lipschitz收缩映射，并在谱比较与几何刚性方面取得重要进展，为相关猜想提供了证明。

Abstract: We use inverse mean curvature flow to construct normalized-area-preserving Lipschitz contractions, in an approach analogous in spirit to the heat-flow construction of Kim and E. Milman. This yields contractions from the round sphere onto closed strictly convex hypersurfaces in the sphere, and from the flat disk onto strictly convex free-boundary hypersurfaces in the Euclidean ball.
  In dimension two, this proves E. Milman's contraction conjecture for Riemannian two-spheres and gives an analogous intrinsic result for nonnegatively curved disks whose boundary has geodesic curvature one. These maps also yield two-sided spectral comparison and map-level rigidity at the actual Lipschitz threshold, recovering a theorem of Lin, Wang and Xu.

</details>


### [123] [A Varifold-Based Score for Decoupling Deformations in Shape Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27808)
*Rayane Mouhli,Barbara Gris,Irène Kaltenmark*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型的耦合分数（coupling score），用于在计算解剖学的形状配准过程中解耦结构化运动和自由微分同胚等不同形变模式，避免高维形变吸收低维成分的问题。该分数基于varifolds的一阶变分定义，并从理论上分析了其在有限维空间上的行为，最后将其作为惩罚项集成到配准问题中。数值实验展示了其在强制/阻止特定运动及迭代修正复杂匹配场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算解剖学中分析形状群体的形态变异性时，通常需要多组件形变模型来组合结构化运动和不受约束的微分同胚。然而，配准过程中高维形变倾向于吸收低维分量，这会改变真实的几何变异性并妨碍准确的统计分析。因此，需要一种机制在配准过程中解耦不同的形变模式。

Method: 1) 引入一种新的耦合分数（coupling score），用于衡量给定向量场对形状的作用可被另一个向量场子空间复制的程度；2) 该分数使用varifolds的一阶变分来定义，即向量场对形状的无穷小作用的varifold表示；3) 在有限维空间上对耦合分数进行理论分析，阐述其行为特性；4) 将该分数作为惩罚项集成到配准问题中，用于强制或防止特定运动。

Result: 数值实验证明了该耦合分数在多种场景下的有效性，包括：强制或阻止特定运动的能力，以及通过强制结构化方向先验来迭代修正复杂匹配场景的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的耦合分数为多组件形变配准中解耦形变模式提供了一种理论有据且实用的工具，能够改善形状种群统计分析的准确性，并为复杂配准场景提供可控的先验约束机制。

Abstract: In computational anatomy, analyzing morphological variability across shape populations often requires multi-component deformation models that combine structured motions and unconstrained diffeomorphisms. However, a major challenge arises during the registration process, as high-dimensional deformations tend to absorb lower-dimensional components, altering the true geometric variability and preventing accurate statistical analysis. To address this issue, we introduce a novel coupling score designed to decouple distinct deformation modes during registration. This score is defined using first variation of varifolds which is a varifold representation of the infinitesimal action of vector fields on shapes. The proposed score quantifies the extent to which the action of a given vector field on a shape can be replicated by another subspace of vector fields. We provide a theoretical analysis of this coupling score, illustrating its behavior on finite-dimensional spaces. Finally, we integrate this score as a penalization term in registration problems. Numerical experiments illustrate its efficiency in various use cases such as enforcing or preventing specific motions, and iteratively correcting complex matching scenarios by enforcing structured directional priors.

</details>


### [124] [Compactness and Willmore Energy of Helicoidal Minimal Surfaces in the 3-Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27965)
*Jianquan Ge,Shilin Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Castro等人提出的关于四维球面S^3中双参数螺旋极小曲面族Hel_c^h的紧致性问题，给出了Hel_c^h为紧致曲面的充要条件（涉及有理性和显式积分q(h,c)的有理性），并完全刻画了商曲面的拓扑类型（环面或Klein瓶），同时显式计算了对应的Willmore能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Castro等人在引入双参数螺旋极小曲面族Hel_c^h后指出，判断该族中哪些成员是紧致曲面并非易事。解决这一紧致性问题是本文的核心动机，因为紧致极小曲面在S^3中具有重要的几何意义（如Clifford环面和Otsuki环面都是该族的紧致成员）。

Method: 作者通过分析参数化曲面的周期性质和全自同构群的作用来判定紧致性。对于c=0的情形，紧致性等价于螺距h为有理数；对于0<c<1/2的情形，需要h为有理数且显式积分q(h,c)也为有理数。通过考察参数平面的商空间，确定了商曲面的拓扑类型（当j和ν均为奇数时为环面，否则为Klein瓶），并利用显式积分计算Willmore能量。

Result: 获得了紧致性的完整刻画：c=0时紧致当且仅当h∈Q；0<c<1/2时紧致当且仅当h∈Q且q(h,c)∈Q。对0<c<1/2，每个紧致商曲面都是环面；对c=0，h=j/ν（最简分数），当j和ν均为奇数时商曲面为环面，否则为Klein瓶。所有紧致浸入曲面的Willmore能量被显式算出。此外，沿每个球面悬链面的Lawson伴生族，仅有有限多个参数值能给出Willmore能量低于任意给定界限的紧致螺旋曲面。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Hel_c^h族的紧致性判定问题，将该族中的紧致成员分为两类：一类是拓扑为环面的紧致螺旋曲面（c=0且h=j/ν，j和ν均为奇数），另一类是拓扑为Klein瓶的紧致螺旋曲面（c=0且j和ν不全为奇数），以及0<c<1/2情形下全部为环面的紧致曲面。这些结果深化了对S^3中极小曲面族结构的理解，并为Willmore能量的研究提供了新的精确计算例。

Abstract: Recently, I. Castro, I. Castro-Infantes, and J. Castro-Infantes introduced a two-parameter family of helicoidal minimal surfaces in $\mathbb S^3$, denoted by $\operatorname{Hel}_c^h$, with the pitch $h\geq0$ and $c\in[0,1/2)$. At $(h,c)=(0,0)$, the surface $\operatorname{Hel}_0^0$ is the totally geodesic sphere, while the limiting surface as $c\to1/2^-$ is the Clifford torus. The subfamily $c=0$, $h>0$, consists of the Lawson spherical helicoids, whereas the subfamily $h=0$, $0<c<1/2$, consists of the spherical catenoids, whose compact members are the Otsuki tori. Castro et al. remarked that it is not an easy problem to determine when $\operatorname{Hel}_c^h$ is a compact surface.
  In this paper, we resolve this compactness problem, namely we prove that the compact members of the family are characterized by \[ \operatorname{Hel}_c^h \text{ is compact} \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad \begin{cases} h\in\mathbb Q, & c=0,\\[1mm] h\in\mathbb Q\ \text{and}\ q(h,c)\in\mathbb Q, & 0<c<1/2, \end{cases} \] where $q(h,c)$ is given by an explicit integral. For $0<c<1/2$, every compact quotient surface induced by the parametrization is a torus. For $c=0$ and $h=j/ν>0$ written in lowest terms, the quotient of the parameter plane by the full automorphism group is a torus when $j$ and $ν$ are both odd and a Klein bottle otherwise. The Willmore energies of the corresponding compact immersed surfaces are computed explicitly. Along each Lawson associated family of a spherical catenoid, only finitely many parameter values yield compact helicoidal surfaces with Willmore energy below any prescribed bound.

</details>


### [125] [$L^p$ Liouville theorems for pluriharmonic functions on gradient Kähler-Ricci solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28057)
*Guangwen Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了完备梯度Kähler-Ricci孤立子上实值多重调和函数的Liouville型定理。在梯度可积性假设下，通过引入由孤立子势诱导的全局全纯量，作者证明了在稳态情形下当∫|∇u|^p dv<∞（0<p<∞）时u必为常数，在收缩情形下当0<p≤2时结论同样成立，并构造反例说明p<1的扩展本质上依赖于孤立子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重调和函数在完备流形上的Liouville性质是几何分析中的经典问题，但相对于调和函数的研究，多重调和函数在Kähler-Ricci孤立子上的结果较为有限。作者希望利用梯度Kähler-Ricci孤立子特有的结构，突破已有调和函数Liouville定理的范围，获得更广泛的可积性条件下的常数性结论。

Method: 作者在梯度Kähler-Ricci孤立子上引入一个由孤立子势f诱导的全局定义的解析（全纯）量，利用该全纯量的性质结合梯度可积性假设，对实值多重调和函数u进行分析。分别处理稳态（steady）和收缩（shrinking）两种情形，并构造具体的完备Kähler流形反例来验证结论的sharp性。

Result: 在稳态梯度Kähler-Ricci孤立子上，若∫|∇u|^p dv<∞（0<p<∞），则u必为常数；在收缩情形下，当0<p≤2时同样可推出u为常数。此外构造了一个完备Kähler流形上的例子，表明0<p<1范围的推广依赖于孤立子结构，在一般完备Kähler流形上并不成立。

Conclusion: 作者获得的Liouville型定理在梯度可积性假设下扩展到了超越调和函数所能达到的范围，揭示了梯度Kähler-Ricci孤立子结构对多重调和函数常数性的关键作用，并通过反例说明该结论在一般完备Kähler流形上不能推广，从而凸显了孤立子结构的本质性。

Abstract: We study Liouville-type theorems for real-valued pluriharmonic functions on complete gradient Kähler-Ricci solitons under gradient integrability assumptions. For a complete gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton $(M,g,J,f)$ and a real-valued pluriharmonic function $u$, we investigate conditions under which $u$ must be constant. By introducing a globally defined holomorphic quantity induced by the soliton potential, we obtain new Liouville-type results beyond the range available for harmonic functions. In the steady case, we prove that $u$ is constant whenever
  $$
  \int_M|\nabla u|^p\mathrm{d}v<\infty
  $$ for some $0<p<\infty$. In the shrinking case, we prove the same conclusion for $0<p\leq 2$. Finally, we construct a complete Kähler example showing that the extension to the range $0<p<1$ relies essentially on the soliton structure and does not hold on general complete Kähler manifolds.

</details>


### [126] [Obstructions to Spin(7) Nahm transforms on tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28303)
*Spencer Whitehead*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究8维环面上带Spin(7)结构的广义Nahm变换。作者构建了具有正负手性Dirac核的瞬子丛，证明通常的Nahm变换在8维情形下不良定义；随后定义了高次幂瞬子线丛扭曲下瞬子的渐近全纯概念，证明该渐近全纯在k的二阶项下约化为Spin(7)，并提供渐近全纯为u(1)^4（而非Spin(7)）的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Nahm变换是4维平坦超凯勒环面T^4上ASD瞬子模空间与对偶环面上瞬子模空间之间的等距，在4维情形下非常有效。自然的问题是将其推广到更高维度，特别是8维环面上带Spin(7)结构的广义Nahm变换。该研究旨在探索Nahm变换在高维推广中的适用性及困难。

Method: 首先在8维环面上构建具有正负手性Dirac核的瞬子丛，检验Nahm变换的可定义性；随后引入渐近全纯的概念——对瞬子线丛的高次幂k（k>>1）进行扭曲，研究其Wilson圈或平行输运的渐近行为；通过二阶展开分析渐近全纯是否保持Spin(7)结构，并构造反例。

Result: 证明了8维Spin(7)环面上通常的Nahm变换定义不良（正负手性Dirac核导致问题）；建立了渐近全纯在k的二阶近似下能约化为Spin(7)的结论；但同时构造了渐近全纯为u(1)^4的代数的具体例子，表明渐近全纯不一定保持Spin(7)结构。

Conclusion: 广义Nahm变换在8维Spin(7)环面上并非简单成立：通常的Nahm变换不适配，而渐近全纯虽然在一阶近似下保持Spin(7)结构，但存在反例使其退化为更对称的u(1)^4结构。这表明高维Nahm变换需要更精细的框架或修正。

Abstract: The Nahm transform for 4-dimensional flat hyperkahler tori is an isometry between the moduli space of anti-self-dual (ASD) instantons on a torus $T^4$ and the moduli space of ASD instantons on the dual torus $\hat{T^4}$ parametrising flat line bundles on $T^4$. This paper studies a generalised Nahm transform on an 8-dimensional torus with a Spin(7) structure. I construct instanton bundles with Dirac kernels respectively in positive and negative chiralities, demonstrating that the usual Nahm transform is not well-defined. I then define a notion of asymptotic holonomy for instantons twisted by a high power $k \gg 1$ of an instanton line bundle, and I show that this asymptotic holonomy reduces to Spin(7) to second order in $k$. Finally, I provide examples for which the asymptotic holonomy is $\mathfrak{u}(1)^4$, and thus not Spin(7).

</details>


### [127] [SU(3)-structures on quotients of 3-Sasakian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28337)
*Quentin Peres,Dimitrios Tsimpis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了7维准正则3-Sasakian orbifold沿其一个Reeb向量场做商所得到的流形Z上的SU(3)-结构，该结构由两个实参数参数化。文章计算了该结构的挠率，证明了它是LT-结构（Lie代数型结构），并在特定参数取值下得到近Kähler结构作为特例，同时给出了正则、准正则及orbifold情形的若干例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3-Sasakian流形是重要的带几何结构的流形，其沿Reeb向量场的商流形在几何与物理学中广泛出现。作者旨在系统构造由3-Sasakian orbifold退化得到的SU(3)-结构，并厘清其挠率类型与近Kähler结构之间的联系，从而丰富齐性和非齐性SU(3)-结构的例子库。

Method: 该方法从7维准正则3-Sasakian orbifold出发，沿其中一个Reeb向量场取商，在商空间Z上利用原结构的Sasakian三元组构造出一个由两个实参数刻画的SU(3)-结构。随后通过直接计算该结构的挠率（torsion）来判定其结构类型，证明其属于LT-结构，并通过调节参数将构造退化到近Kähler情形。

Result: 作者证明商空间Z上的SU(3)-结构是LT-结构（即挠率属于特定的Lie代数表示子空间），且该结构族包含近Kähler结构作为参数取特殊值的特例。文中提供了正则、准正则以及orbifold情形下的一系列具体例子。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种从3-Sasakian orbifold系统构造LT-型SU(3)-结构与近Kähler结构的一般方法，统一并推广了先前关于此类构造的已知结果，为理解SU(3)-结构的挠率分类及其几何意义提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We show that if $(S,g,ξ_i,η_i,Φ_i)$ is a quasi-regular $3$-Sasakian orbifold of dimension $7$, then its quotient $Z$ by one of the Reeb vector fields inherits an $SU(3)$-structure parametrized by two real parameters. We compute its torsion and show that it is an LT-structure. Moreover, for certain values of the parameters, we can provide a nearly Kähler structure on $Z$ appearing as a special case of our construction. We give several examples in the regular, quasi-regular and orbifold cases.

</details>


### [128] [The space of metrics with positive generalized conformal Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28467)
*Gioacchino Antonelli,Georg Frenck,Bernhard Hanke*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究闭连通光滑流形上广义共形拉普拉斯算子严格正定时度量空间R^γ(M)的同伦性质。证明了在n=2且γ≥0，或n≥3且0≤γ≤4(n-1)/(n-2)时，映射R^0(M)↪R^γ(M)是同伦等价；当n≥3且γ>4(n-1)/(n-2)时，R^γ(M)可缩。这推广了Botvinnik-Rosenberg和Li-Mantoulidis的结果，并在最大系数范围内解决了Gromov猜想的一个同伦论强化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov在"Four Lectures on Scalar Curvature"中提出了关于标量曲率相关度量空间拓扑结构的猜想（猜想3，第6.1.2节）。作者旨在从同伦等价的角度强化并解决该猜想，同时推广前人在二维情形和特定参数范围的结果到所有维度和最大参数区间。

Method: 文章采用同伦论与几何分析的交叉方法。关键证明工具包括：对于R^0(M)到R^γ(M)的同伦等价的证明，利用了在最大参数范围内算子的正定性保持性质；对于高参数情形（γ>4(n-1)/(n-2)）的空间可缩性，构造了显式的收缩映射或通过路径连通性论证。同时作者处理了等变情形（带群作用的流形）以及带边流形的情形。

Result: 主要结果有二：(1) 当n=2且γ≥0，或n≥3且0≤γ≤4(n-1)/(n-2)时，包含映射R^0(M)↪R^γ(M)是同伦等价；(2) 当n≥3且γ>4(n-1)/(n-2)时，R^γ(M)是可缩的，因此非空。此外还得到了等变情形和带边流形情形的对应结果。

Conclusion: 作者在最大可能的系数范围内解决了Gromov猜想的一个同伦论强化版本，将先前关于二维情形的结果全面推广到所有维数，并补充了等变和带边流形的情形，为标量曲率和共形几何中度量空间的拓扑研究提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $n\geq2$ and let $M^n$ be a closed connected smooth manifold. Let $R^γ(M)$ be the space of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$ on $M$ for which the generalized conformal Laplace operator $-γΔ_g+\mathrm{R}_g$ is strictly positive.
  We prove that if $n=2$ and $γ\ge0$, or if $n\ge3$ and $0\leq γ\leq 4(n-1)/(n-2)$, the inclusion $R^0(M)\hookrightarrow R^γ(M)$ is a homotopy equivalence, thus generalizing, to all dimensions and in the maximal range, the results of Botvinnik--Rosenberg and Li--Mantoulidis. Then, we prove that if $n\ge3$ and $γ>4(n-1)/(n-2)$, the space $R^γ(M)$ is contractible, and hence nonempty. This solves a homotopy-theoretic strengthening of a conjecture of Gromov (Conjecture 3, Section 6.1.2, "Four Lectures on Scalar Curvature") in the maximal possible coefficient range.
  Concerning Gromov's conjecture we also treat the equivariant case and the case of manifolds with boundary.

</details>


### [129] [Second Eigenvalue Estimates and Spectral Rigidity for Submanifolds of Compact Rank-One Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28508)
*Márcio Batista,Abraão Mendes*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致射影空间（实、复、四元数及Cayley射影平面）中k维闭子流形上薛定谔算子L=Δ+|σ|²+k的第二特征值的上界估计。通过结合标准球面嵌入与共形检验函数方法，在复、四元数和Cayley情形下给出了包含切空间位置修正项的上界，并推导了仅依赖于子流形维数和除代数类型的普适估计。等号成立条件迫使子流形为完全脐点；借助已知分类，计算了标准模型的第二特征值并识别了达到锐上界的全测地例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为紧致射影空间中的闭子流形上的薛定谔算子建立第二特征值的上界估计，并探讨等号成立时的几何刚性，揭示特征值与子流形几何结构（如切空间相对位置、脐性等）之间的内在联系。

Method: 结合目标空间的球面嵌入（spherical embedding）与共形检验函数（conformal test-function）技巧来构造测试函数，从而推导特征值上界。在复、四元数及Cayley情形下，修正项记录了子流形切平面相对于对应几何结构（如复结构、四元数结构）的位置信息。同时利用完全脐点子流形的已知分类来识别等号成立情形。

Result: 得到了四类紧致射影空间中闭子流形上薛定谔算子第二特征值的上界；在复、四元数和Cayley情形中上界含几何修正项，且推导出仅依赖维数与基础除代数的普适估计。证明了估计的锐性（sharpness）在等号时迫使子流形完全脐点。并基于完全脐点子流形分类，计算了标准实、复、四元数及Cayley模型的第二特征值，识别出达到锐上界的全测地子流形。

Conclusion: 成功建立了紧致射影空间中闭子流形薛定谔算子第二特征值的（带修正项的）上界，阐明了等号与完全脐点/全测地子流形的几何刚性关系，并通过已知分类给出了具体达到上界的模型例子。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish upper bounds for the second eigenvalue of the Schrödinger operator $L=Δ+|σ|^2+k$ on $k$-dimensional closed submanifolds of the compact projective spaces $\mathbb F P^m$, where $\mathbb F\in\{\mathbb R,\mathbb C,\mathbb H\}$, as well as the Cayley projective plane. The estimates are obtained by combining the standard spherical embeddings of these spaces with a conformal test-function argument. In the complex, quaternionic, and Cayley cases, the resulting bounds involve correction terms that record the position of the tangent spaces of the submanifold relative to the corresponding geometric structures. We also derive universal estimates depending only on the dimension of the submanifold and the underlying division algebra. We show that equality in the sharp estimates forces the submanifold to be totally umbilical. Finally, using known classifications of totally umbilical submanifolds, we compute the second eigenvalue for the standard real, complex, quaternionic, and Cayley models and identify the totally geodesic examples that attain the sharp upper bounds.

</details>


### [130] [Completeness of the Model Space Does Not Force Regularity for Infinite-Dimensional Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28557)
*Zongjian Han,Fungo Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，解决了无限维李理论中一个长期悬而未决的基本问题：并非所有以完备局部凸空间为模型的李群都是正则的。该反例是一个可缩的复解析BCH–李群H，以完备Silva空间为模型，其指数映射是同胚，但H甚至不是C⁰-半正则的，同时该构造还否定了Glöckner–Neeb关于乘法增长条件的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维李理论中长期存在的开放问题：是否每个以完备局部凸空间为模型的李群都是正则的（regular）。此前已有反例表明非完备情形下不成立，但完备情形下该问题一直悬而未决。此外，Glöckner–Neeb提出的乘法增长条件是否为Mackey-完备连续逆代数的自动性质也是一个待解答的问题。

Method: 构造一个以完备Silva空间φ_ℂ为模型的复解析BCH–李群H，该群是某个完备复连续逆代数的单位群主分支。作者通过精心设计的代数结构，使得该群的指数映射为同胚，但构造合适的控制函数（在某个固定有限维子空间中趋于零），使其不存在H值的C¹演化，从而证明该群不满足正则性乃至C⁰-半正则性。该构造同时检验了乘法增长条件，在完备性假设下给出反例。

Result: 成功构造出一个可缩的复解析BCH–李群H，其指数映射是同胚，但该群不是C⁰-半正则的，因此更不是正则的。具体地，存在光滑控制函数（在固定有限维子空间中趋于零），在[0,1)上有唯一光滑演化，但在[0,1]上没有C¹演化。该构造同时否定了Glöckner–Neeb关于完备连续逆代数乘法增长条件的猜想。

Conclusion: 该反例彻底否定了"以完备局部凸空间为模型的李群必为正则"的猜想，表明完备性本身不足以保证无限维李群的正则性，即使是指数映射为同胚的"良好"群也可能不满足正则性。此外，该工作还回答了Glöckner–Neeb问题，说明其乘法增长条件在完备情形下也不是自动成立的。

Abstract: We construct a counterexample resolving a fundamental open problem in infinite-dimensional Lie theory: whether every Lie group modelled on a complete locally convex space is regular. The example is a contractible complex analytic BCH--Lie group $H$, modelled on the complete Silva space $\varphi_{\mathbb C}$, whose exponential map is a homeomorphism, yet $H$ is not even $C^0$-semiregular. Smooth controls tending to zero in one fixed finite-dimensional subspace have no $H$-valued $C^1$ evolution, although each has a unique smooth evolution on $[0,1)$. The group is the principal unit group of a complete complex continuous inverse algebra. The construction also answers negatively the Glöckner--Neeb question whether their multiplication-growth condition is automatic for Mackey-complete continuous inverse algebras, even under the stronger assumption of completeness.

</details>

### [131] [Types and collapse for cuspidal representations of groups acting on trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27590)
*Samuel Johnson,Martin H. Weissman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文是对前一篇论文的深化与细化，在秩一p-adic群上证明了每个超尖尖（supercuspidal）表示由紧模中心开子群诱导，并提供了两个改进：一是通过几何极小的K型对诱导数据进行几何描述；二是证明了等变层塌缩到内射层。这两个概念可推广到高秩群，并提出了两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前作中，作者证明了秩一p-adic群的每个超尖尖表示都由紧模中心开子群诱导，但采用的是几何方法（局部化表示以获得树上的等变层）。本文的动机是进一步细化这一结果：一方面希望更精确地描述诱导数据的几何结构，另一方面希望理解等变层的代数结构性质（塌缩为内射层），并探索这些性质向高秩群的推广。

Method: 本文采用几何方法，继续沿用以树为几何工具的局部化技术。具体而言，第一项改进通过对诱导数据给出几何极小的K型描述，刻画了具有最小几何支持的数据；第二项改进证明了等变层可塌缩到内射层，这涉及层论中的代数结构分析。这两种概念（几何极小K型与可塌缩性）被推广到高秩情形。

Result: 作者获得了两项具体成果：(1) 给出了诱导数据的几何描述，通过几何极小的K型来刻画；(2) 证明了等变层可以塌缩到内射层。此外，这两个技术概念被证明可以自然推广到高秩群的情形，从而引出两个待证的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文在秩一情形完成了对超尖尖表示诱导结构的两项几何细化，并且指出几何极小K型和等变层塌缩性这两个更一般的概念在高秩群上同样适用，为后续研究（两个猜想）奠定了基础。

Abstract: In a previous paper, the second author proved that every supercuspidal representation of a rank-one p-adic group is induced from a compact-mod-center open subgroup. The method was geometric, localizing representations to obtain equivariant sheaves on trees. Here we provide two refinements. The first is a geometric description of the inducing data, via a geometrically minimal K-type. Second is a proof that the equivariant sheaves collapse onto injective sheaves. The two notions of geometrically minimal K-types and collapsibility generalize to higher rank groups, suggesting a pair of conjectures.

</details>


### [132] [On weight modules over truncated shifted iYangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27596)
*Yaolong Shen,Linliang Song,Rui Xiong*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入与带对合箭图相关的定向KLRW（oKLRW）代数，构造其忠实多项式表示和图示基，并通过双反射KLR图定义KLR iYangians，实现iGKLO同态的图示化。对整数参数，引入区间oKLRW代数，证明截断移位iYangians的整数权模与对应区间oKLRW代数的幂零模之间的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断移位iYangians是一族期望量子化仿射Grassmannian切片某些分量的代数，需要建立其与KLR型代数的联系以及相应的表示论框架。

Method: 引入带对合箭图的oKLRW代数并证明其存在忠实多项式表示与图示基；利用双反射KLR图定义KLR iYangians，构造iGKLO同态的图示实现；对整数参数定义区间oKLRW代数并建立其与截断移位iYangians之间的模范畴等价。

Result: 建立了oKLRW代数的忠实多项式表示和图示基，给出iGKLO同态的图示实现，并证明整数参数下截断移位iYangians的整数权模范畴与区间oKLRW代数幂零模范畴之间的等价性。

Conclusion: oKLRW代数为截断移位iYangians提供了图示化和表示论工具，区间oKLRW代数与截断移位iYangians之间的模范畴等价为其表示理论奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Truncated shifted iYangians are a family of algebras expected to quantize certain components of affine Grassmannian islices. We introduce orientifold KLRW (oKLRW) algebras associated with quivers with involution and establish their faithful polynomial representations and diagrammatic bases. We also define KLR iYangians using double reflective KLR diagrams and construct diagrammatic realizations of the iGKLO homomorphisms. For integral parameters, we introduce interval oKLRW algebras and prove an equivalence between integral weight modules over truncated shifted iYangians and nilpotent modules over the corresponding interval oKLRW algebras.

</details>


### [133] [The ordered F-system for the F-method and differential symmetry breaking operators for $(GL(3,\mathbb{R}), GL(2,\mathbb{R}))$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27956)
*Jonathan Ditlevsen,Toshihisa Kubo,Víctor Pérez-Valdés*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入F-method中当幂零根不一定可交换时F-system的新方面，定义了对称化算子，并由此得到有序F-system。作为应用，分类并构造了$(GL(3,\mathbb{R}), GL(2,\mathbb{R}))$之间主系列表示的所有微分对称破缺算子（DSBOs）的对称化形式和有序形式，并进一步研究了相关的微分交织算子及Verma模的分支律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的F-method在处理DSBOs时通常假设幂零根可交换，需要将其推广到幂零根非可交换的情形，以统一处理DSBOs的对称化形式和有序形式。

Method: 在有限维李代数$\mathfrak{g}$的对偶空间$\mathfrak{g}^\vee$上的多项式函数空间中定义对称化算子；通过对F-system进行该算子的共轭得到有序F-system；利用这两种技术统一研究DSBOs的对称化形式和有序形式。

Result: 分类并构造了$(GL(3,\mathbb{R}), GL(2,\mathbb{R}))$之间所有嵌入（对应$\mathfrak{gl}(3,\mathbb{R})$的正根）的主系列表示的DSBOs的对称化与有序形式；将其应用于研究$GL(3,\mathbb{R})$的微分交织算子（DIOs）和$(SL(3,\mathbb{R}), SL(2,\mathbb{R}))$的DSBOs，并通过Verma模的分支律验证了结果。

Conclusion: 有序F-system与对称化算子提供了处理非可交换幂零根情形下F-method的统一框架，使DSBOs的对称化与有序形式能够被统一分类和构造，并在此框架下完成了$(GL(3,\mathbb{R}), GL(2,\mathbb{R}))$及相关对的具体分类结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a new aspect of the F-system for the F-method arising in the case where the nilpotent radical is not necessarily abelian. For this, we define the symmetrization operator on the space of polynomial functions on the dual $\mathfrak{g}^\vee$ of a finite-dimensional Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$. In the context of the F-method, the conjugation by this operator of the F-system yields a new system, which we call the ordered F-system. These two techniques allow one to consider the symmetrized form (with symmetrization) and ordered form (without symmetrization) of differential symmetry breaking operators (DSBOs) uniformly.
  As an application of this theory, we classify and construct all the DSBOs between principal series representations for $(GL(3,\mathbb{R}), GL(2,\mathbb{R}))$ in both symmetrized and ordered forms. Here, we consider all embeddings $GL(2,\mathbb{R}) \hookrightarrow GL(3,\mathbb{R})$ corresponding to the positive roots of $\mathfrak{gl}(3,\mathbb{R})$. Furthermore, we utilize the DSBOs for the above pair to investigate differential intertwining operators (DIOs) for $GL(3,\mathbb{R})$ and DSBOs for $(SL(3,\mathbb{R}), SL(2,\mathbb{R}))$. The branching laws of the corresponding Verma modules are also studied to support our results of DSBOs.

</details>


### [134] [On a proof of the Gorenstein Symmetry Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28011)
*Weiqing Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文通过同调方法证明了著名的Gorenstein对称猜想，即Artin代数的自内射维数单侧有限性足以保证其Gorenstein性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gorenstein对称猜想是代数表示论与同调代数领域的著名猜想，断言Artin代数的自内射维数如果单侧有限（即一侧有限），则该代数必定是Gorenstein的。证明这一猜想对于理解Artin代数的同调性质具有重要意义。

Method: 采用同调代数的证明方法（homological proof），利用同调工具对Artin代数的自内射维数进行系统性分析，从而建立单侧有限性与Gorenstein性之间的逻辑关联。

Result: 成功证明了Gorenstein对称猜想成立，即Artin代数自内射维数的单侧有限性足以保证其Gorenstein性。

Conclusion: 该论文为Gorenstein对称猜想提供了同调证明，填补了该领域在Artin代数Gorenstein性质研究方面的空白，对后续相关研究具有重要的理论价值。

Abstract: We give a homological proof of the famous Gorenstein Symmetry Conjecture, which asserts that one-sided finiteness of the self-injective dimension of an Artin algebra is sufficient for Gorensteinness.

</details>


### [135] [Classification of Simple Cuspidal Modules over Nongraded Witt Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28114)
*Genqiang Liu,Xiaoyao Zheng,Yufang Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Weyl型代数D_n与一般线性李代数gl_n相关的张量模T(P,V)的不可约性，并证明A_n#U(g_n)同构于D_n⊗U(m_{1,0}Δ)，进而证明所有简单尖点g_n-模同构于某个T(A_n(λ),V)的简单商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非交换代数上李代数g_n的模表示理论，特别是确定张量模T(P,V)何时为单模，以及分类简单尖点g_n-模块，这是李代数表示论中的重要问题。

Method: 首先确定T(P,V)=P⊗V为单模的条件（P为Weyl型代数D_n的简单模，V为gl_n的简单模）；然后证明代数同构A_n#U(g_n)≅D_n⊗U(m_{1,0}Δ)；利用该同构将简单尖点g_n-模实现为张量模T(A_n(λ),V)的简单商。

Result: 给出了张量模T(P,V)为单模的判定条件；建立了规范代数同构A_n#U(g_n)≅D_n⊗U(m_{1,0}Δ)；证明了每个简单尖点g_n-模都同构于某个T(A_n(λ),V)的简单商，其中V为有限维简单gl_n-模。

Conclusion: 通过建立代数同构和单模判定准则，完成了对简单尖点g_n-模的结构分类，揭示了其与Weyl型代数D_n和张量构造之间的深层联系。

Abstract: For a positive integer $n$, let $A_n=\mathbb{C}[t_1^{\pm1},\ldots,t_n^{\pm1},x_1,\ldots,x_n]$ and $\mathfrak{g}_n=\bigoplus_{i=1}^n A_nd_i$, where $d_i=t_i\frac{\partial}{\partial t_i} +\frac{\partial}{\partial x_i}$. We first determine when the tensor module $T(P,V)=P\otimes V$ is simple, where $P$ is a simple module over the Weyl type algebra $D_n$ and $V$ is a simple $\mathfrak{gl}_n$-module. We then prove a canonical algebra isomorphism $A_n\#U(\mathfrak{g}_n)\cong D_n\otimes U(\mathfrak{m}_{\mathbf{1},\mathbf{0}}Δ)$, and use it to show that every simple cuspidal $\mathfrak{g}_n$-module is isomorphic to a simple quotient of some $T(A_n(λ),V)$, where $V$ is a finite-dimensional simple $\mathfrak{gl}_n$-module.

</details>


### [136] [Higher-level degenerate spin affine Hecke superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28228)
*Thomas Moran*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文定义了一类新的超代数——高层退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数，并研究了其结构理论，建立了其与高层退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数之间的同构关系，这是已有退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数与退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数之间关系的高层类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数与退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数之间已知关系的基础上，作者希望将其推广到更高层级（higher-level）的情形，从而丰富超代数结构理论的研究。

Method: 定义新的高层退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数类，通过建立同构映射将其与高层退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数联系起来，利用结构理论研究两个超代数之间的关系。

Result: 成功建立了高层退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数与高层退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数之间的同构关系，证明了两者的等价性。

Conclusion: 该同构关系是此前退化自旋仿射Hecke超代数与退化仿射Hecke-Clifford超代数之间关系的高层级推广，为后续相关超代数结构理论的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We define a new class of superalgebras, called higher-level degenerate spin affine Hecke superalgebras, and study their structure theory. We establish an isomorphism that relates the higher-level degenerate spin affine Hecke superalgebras to the higher-level degenerate affine Hecke-Clifford superalgebras. The relationship between these superalgebras is a higher-level analogue of the relationship between the degenerate spin affine Hecke superalgebras and degenerate affine Hecke-Clifford superalgebras.

</details>


### [137] [A Tale of Two Idempotents: Casselman-Shalika and Spherical Genericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28354)
*Yi Luo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了Casselman-Shalika公式和Li的一般性准则的纯Hecke代数推导，完全绕开了非分歧主序列和交织算子，仅利用Iwahori-Hecke代数中的球面和符号幂等元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，Casselman-Shalika公式和Li的genericity准则的证明依赖于非分歧主序列和交织算子等较为复杂的工具。作者希望提供一个更自洽、更直接的推导路径，以Hecke代数本身的结构（幂等元及其双边模）为核心来完成证明。

Method: 方法的核心是Iwahori-Hecke代数H中的两个幂等元：球面幂等元e_K和符号幂等元e_sgn。两者的左理想He_K=Ae_K和He_sgn=Ae_sgn在Bernstein子代数A上是秩一自由模。通过描述e_KHe_K在Ae_K中的嵌入恢复Satake同构；描述e_KHe_sgn在Ae_sgn中的嵌入得到Gelfand-Graev表示的K-不变量的秩一自由性及Casselman-Shalika公式；对称地，描述e_sgnHe_K在Ae_K中的嵌入判定球面模的符号等型部分是否非零，从而得到Li的一般性准则。

Result: 成功以自洽的Hecke代数方式重现了Satake同构、Casselman-Shalika公式和Li的genericity准则，且不依赖非分歧主序列或交织算子。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了Iwahori-Hecke代数中幂等元之间的双边模结构足以统一地导出若干核心的表示论结果，提供了一种比传统方法更直接、更自洽的推导框架。

Abstract: We give a self-contained Hecke-algebraic derivation of the Casselman-Shalika formula and J.-S. Li's genericity criterion for irreducible spherical representations of unramified groups, without using the unramified principal series or intertwining operators. The argument centers on the spherical and sign idempotents $e_K$ and $e_{\mathrm{sgn}}$ of the Iwahori-Hecke algebra $\mathcal{H}$. Their left ideals $\mathcal{H}e_K=\mathcal{A}e_K$ and $\mathcal{H}e_{\mathrm{sgn}}=\mathcal{A}e_{\mathrm{sgn}}$ are free of rank one over the Bernstein subalgebra $\mathcal{A}$. Describing $e_K\mathcal{H}e_K$ inside $\mathcal{A}e_K$ recovers the Satake isomorphism. Describing $e_K\mathcal{H}e_{\mathrm{sgn}}$ inside $\mathcal{A}e_{\mathrm{sgn}}$ yields rank-one freeness of the $K$-invariants of the Gelfand-Graev representation and the Casselman-Shalika formula. Symmetrically, describing $e_{\mathrm{sgn}}\mathcal{H}e_K$ inside $\mathcal{A}e_K$ determines when the sign-isotypic part of a spherical module is non-zero, and hence yields Li's genericity criterion.

</details>
