### [163] [Hubble tension problem encompassed by phase-space quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27285)
*Alex E. Bernardini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Weyl-Wigner量子力学的框架，在广义相空间量子宇宙学背景下重新审视哈勃张量问题，并提出了一种扩展公式，证明局部化相空间量子态的残余量子修正能够抑制早期与晚期宇宙学预测之间的哈勃张量分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张量问题是当前宇宙学中最重要的开放性难题之一——早期宇宙（CMB）与晚期宇宙（距离阶梯）对哈勃常数$H_0$的测量存在显著分歧。作者希望通过量子宇宙学的相空间框架，探索量子效应是否能自然缓解或解决这一张力问题，而不需要引入新的物理或修改现有标准宇宙学模型。

Method: 作者在minisuperspace框架下构建量子宇宙学模型，采用Weyl-Wigner量子力学的相空间方法，使用（广义）局部化相空间量子态描述量子宇宙学。他们从该框架中解析推导出爱因斯坦-弗里德曼方程的残余量子修正项，并检验这些修正对早期与晚期哈勃常数预测分歧的影响。除了标准ΛCDM模型外，该方法还扩展到包含曲率和暗区（暗物质、暗能量）修正的广义量子宇宙学情景。

Result: 解析推导表明，当考虑局部化相空间量子态的残余量子修正时，量子效应能够抑制早期与晚期预测之间的哈勃张量分歧，从而在标准ΛCDM模型框架内缓解哈勃张量问题。该结论还扩展到包含曲率和暗区修正的广义量子宇宙学情景。

Conclusion: Weyl-Wigner量子力学的相空间框架为哈勃张量问题提供了一种新的解决途径：量子修正效应可以自然地压制早期与晚期$H_0$测量之间的张力，且该方法不仅适用于标准ΛCDM模型，也适用于更广泛的广义量子宇宙学场景。

Abstract: Analytical solutions encompassing the so-called Hubble tension problem are revisited through the framework of Weyl--Wigner quantum mechanics and discussed in the context of generalized phase-space scenarios of quantum cosmology. After reviewing the nature of the problem and its recent developments, an extended formulation constructed within the quantum phase-space framework to address the Hubble tension is proposed. For the quantum cosmology described in the minisuperspace framework through (generic) localized phase-space quantum states, when residual quantum corrections to the Einstein--Friedmann equation are analytically derived, quantum effects are shown to suppress the Hubble tension divergence between early- and late-time predictions. Besides addressing the Hubble tension problem within the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model, our approach encompasses generalized quantum cosmological scenarios that also include curvature and dark sector modifications.

</details>


### [164] [Nonlinear Dynamics near the Threshold of Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27343)
*Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟，研究标量场在自引力下坍缩过程中微扰论在平直时空附近展开（包括次次领头阶）的有效性边界。研究发现当峰值光度达到约10^-2普朗克光度时，系统在黑洞形成前就会偏离微扰预测，该非线性效应表现为驱动频率的红移和幂律谱振幅，且与近似离散自相似性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰论是建模和解释引力动力学（如双黑洞并合）的重要工具，精确理解其适用边界至关重要。标量场自引力坍缩为研究微扰论与非线性强引力效应之间的转变提供了一个干净的实验平台。

Method: 采用数值相对论模拟，通过改变标量场的初始振幅，在微扰区域（场近似平直时空中散射）和非微扰区域（有限时间内形成黑洞）之间平滑过渡。具体分析围绕平直时空展开的微扰级数（包含次次领头阶 NNLO 效应）的准确性，并将模拟结果与微扰预测进行定量对比。

Result: 模拟显示，当过程的最大光度足够大（L_peak ~ 10^-2 L_Planck）时，标量场在黑洞形成之前就会偏离微扰预测。这些非线性效应包括驱动频率的红移和幂律谱振幅，且后者与近似离散自相似性（临界坍缩现象）一致。

Conclusion: 该工作进一步明确了微扰论展开的失效临界点（约10^-2普朗克光度），为理解更真实强引力现象（如非球对称坍缩和高速度黑洞并合）中微扰展开的极限提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Perturbation theory is an essential tool to model and interpret gravitational dynamics, for example, binary black hole mergers. Therefore it is also crucial to precisely understand its regimes of validity. The collapse of a scalar field under its own self-gravity provides a clean laboratory to study these questions. By varying the field's initial amplitude we can transition smoothly between a perturbative regime, where the field scatters in an approximately flat spacetime; and a nonperturbative regime, where a black hole forms in finite time. In this work, we use numerical relativity simulations of this set-up to investigate the accuracy of a perturbative expansion around flat spacetime, including next-to-next-to-leading order effects. Our simulations show deviations from these perturbative predictions before black hole formation, once the maximum luminosity of the process is sufficiently large, $L_{peak} \sim 10^{-2} L_{Planck}$. We characterize these nonlinear effects including a redshift of the driving frequency and a power-law spectral amplitude, which we show is consistent with approximate discrete self-similarity. These results provide a step forward towards understanding the limits of perturbative expansions in more realistic strong-gravity phenomena such as non-spherical collapse and high-velocity black hole mergers.

</details>


### [165] [Inflation in unimodular loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27344)
*Steffen Gielen,Rita B. Neves*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了单模圈量子宇宙学中的暴胀模型，该框架使用单模时间而非标准圈量子宇宙学中的无质量标量场作为时间演化变量。单模时间坐标具有清晰的几何意义，与具体物质内容无关，可自然地引入Schrödinger时间演化。作者推导了常数势等简单情形下的解析解，并讨论了二次势和Starobinsky暴胀等数值解。特别地，α-吸引子势可产生动能主导或势能主导的反弹模型，且与观测兼容，可能包含量子引力区域的观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准圈量子宇宙学以自由无质量标量场作为时间钟，这依赖于特定物质内容。单模时间提供了一个不依赖物质内容的、具有明确几何意义的时间坐标，使得暴胀子不必充当时钟，从而在更普遍的框架下研究暴胀和反弹过程。

Method: 采用单模圈量子宇宙学的半经典有效方程进行分析；对常数势等简单情形推导解析解；对二次势、Starobinsky暴胀和α-吸引子势等情形进行数值求解，并比较不同初始条件的选择。

Result: 在单模时间框架下获得了常数势情形的解析解；数值求解了二次势和Starobinsky势的暴胀模型；发现α-吸引子势可以产生动能主导或势能主导的反弹模型，这些模型与观测兼容，并可能包含量子引力区域的观测特征。

Conclusion: 单模圈量子宇宙学提供了不依赖特定物质内容的、几何意义明确的时间框架，可用于暴胀和反弹模型的研究，且α-吸引子势的反弹模型兼具观测兼容性和潜在的量子引力印记可探测性。

Abstract: We study inflation in the setting of unimodular loop quantum cosmology, where time evolution is defined in unimodular time rather than with respect to a free, massless scalar field as is standard in loop quantum cosmology. The unimodular setting leads to a natural Schrödinger time evolution in a time coordinate with clear geometric meaning, defined independently of any particular matter content; an inflaton can be included but is not needed as a clock. We review the unimodular version of loop quantum cosmology and comment on possible connections to full (unimodular) loop quantum gravity. Then, focusing on semiclassical effective equations, we derive analytical solutions in simple cases such as a constant potential, emphasising the use of a unimodular time coordinate. We also discuss numerical solutions for phenomenologically interesting cases such as a quadratic potential and Starobinsky inflation, comparing different possible choices of initial conditions. In particular, we show that choosing an $α$-attractor potential allows for models of a bounce either dominated by kinetic or potential energy, which are compatible with observations while potentially including observable imprints of the quantum-gravity regime.

</details>


### [166] [Gauge vs (hidden) physical symmetries of FLRW cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27351)
*Andrea Calcinari,Adrià Delhom,Federico Greco,Daniele Oriti,Néstor Rivero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究平坦FLRW几何与n个无质量标量场耦合的极小超空间模型中，规范对称与（隐藏的）物理对称之间的关系。结果表明极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成具有Dirac可观测量的守恒荷，其泊松代数为最大共形代数conf(n,1)≃so(n+1,2)。Eisenhart-Duval提升中的显现对称代数依赖于规范选择，在调和规范下放大为薛定谔代数（并非物理对称）。去参数化将提升荷映射为规范不变的Dirac可观测量，始终实现共形代数的子代数，并在调和规范下完整复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般协变理论中，坐标时间演化是规范变换，因此在规范固定描述中显现的对称性不一定是物理动力学的对称性。另一方面，去参数化虽然消除了规范对称，但可能隐藏物理对称性，特别是那些依赖于所选择的物理时钟的对称性。作者旨在建立区分规范对称与物理对称的框架，并恢复约化相空间描述在结构上无法看到的守恒荷。

Method: 作者在平坦FLRW几何与n个自由无质量标量场耦合的模型中进行研究。首先证明极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成规范不变的Dirac可观测量守恒荷，并计算其泊松代数。其次在多个规范族中重新考察Eisenhart-Duval提升，分析规范依赖的显现对称代数。最后通过去参数化将提升荷映射为规范不变的Dirac可观测量，验证其实现共形代数的子代数结构。

Result: （1）极小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量生成守恒电荷，这些电荷是Dirac可观测量（规范不变），其泊松代数构成最大共形代数conf(n,1)≃so(n+1,2)，将先前单标量场结果推广到任意n个标量场。（2）Eisenhart-Duval提升的显现对称代数是规范依赖的：在特殊的调和规范（极小超空间度规范固定后变成平坦）下放大为薛定谔代数，该代数并非物理对称。（3）去参数化将提升荷映射为规范不变的Dirac可观测量，始终实现共形代数的子代数，在调和规范下完整复现共形代数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了在极小超空间模型中分离规范对称与物理对称的框架，能够恢复约化相空间描述在结构上无法识别的守恒荷。该框架在存在势能项的情况下仍然适用，为理解量子引力与背景无关量子理论的对称性结构提供了基础性工具。

Abstract: In generally covariant theories evolution in coordinate time is a gauge transformation, so that a symmetry made manifest in a gauge-fixed description need not be a symmetry of the physical dynamics. Deparametrisation, in turn, removes gauge symmetries but may hide physical symmetries, in particular those dependent on the chosen physical clock. We study the relation between gauge and (hidden) physical symmetries in flat FLRW geometry coupled to an arbitrary number $n$ of free massless scalar fields. We show that conformal Killing vectors of the minisuperspace metric generate conserved charges which are Dirac observables--hence gauge-invariant--and whose Poisson algebra is the maximal conformal algebra $\mathfrak{conf}(n,1)\simeq\mathfrak{so}(n+1,2)$, extending previous single-field results to arbitrary $n$. We then revisit the Eisenhart-Duval lift in a family of gauges and show that the manifest symmetry algebra is gauge dependent, enlarging to the Schrödinger algebra (which is thus not a physical symmetry) in the distinguished harmonic gauge where the gauge-fixed minisuperspace metric becomes flat. Further, deparametrisation maps the lifted charges to gauge-invariant Dirac observables, which always realise a subalgebra of the conformal algebra and reproduce it in full in the harmonic gauge. These results establish a framework for separating gauge from physical symmetries in minisuperspace models, recovering charges to which reduced phase-space descriptions are structurally blind, and remaining applicable in the presence of potentials.

</details>


### [167] [Quark Stars in $f(T,\mathcal{T}) $ Gravity: Structure, Stability, and Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27365)
*Takol Tangphati,Ayan Banerjee,Izzet Sakalli,Sayantan Ghosh,Aseel Smerat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(T,𝒯)引力框架下夸克星的结构。通过MIT袋模型描述夸克物质并求解修正的恒星结构方程，发现最大质量随耦合常数呈非单调变化：在中等正耦合处达到约2.02个太阳质量的峰值，随后在临界耦合附近急剧下降。满足两倍太阳质量脉冲星约束的耦合窗口是有限的，且稳定分支上的所有构型都满足因果性和广义相对论基准的致密性与表面红移限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克星完全由退禁闭夸克物质构成，为检验广义相对论之外的修正引力理论提供了理想的研究对象。作者希望在f(T,𝒯)引力这一遥平行修正理论中研究夸克星结构，探索扭转与能量动量张量迹的耦合如何影响恒星的观测性质（质量、半径、致密性和表面红移）。

Method: 采用f(T,𝒯)引力理论，其中扭转直接与能量-动量张量的迹通过单一常数耦合。使用标准MIT袋模型描述夸克物质，求解修正后的恒星结构方程，并在整个允许的耦合参数范围内系统扫描，考察质量、半径、致密性和表面红移随耦合的变化规律。

Result: 最大质量随耦合呈非单调变化：在中等正耦合处超过广义相对论值并达到约2.02个太阳质量的峰值，随后随耦合趋近临界值（结构方程变得奇异）而急剧下降。两倍太阳质量脉冲星约束仅在有限的正耦合窗口内被满足。稳定分支上所有构型均满足因果性，且低于广义相对论标准致密性和表面红移基准。

Conclusion: f(T,𝒯)引力为夸克星提供了与广义相对论可区分的预测，非单调的最大质量行为揭示了耦合常数的允许区间由观测约束进一步收窄。该修正引力框架能够在满足现有天文约束的同时产生与广义相对论显著不同的恒星结构特征。

Abstract: Quark stars-hypothetical compact stars made entirely of deconfined quark matter-offer a clean testing ground for gravity beyond general relativity. We study their structure in $f(T,\mathcal{T})$ gravity, a teleparallel theory in which torsion is coupled directly to the trace of the energy-momentum tensor through a single constant coupling. Using the standard MIT bag description of quark matter, we solve the modified stellar structure equations and follow how the mass, radius, compactness, and surface redshift respond as the coupling is varied across its full admissible range. The maximum mass turns out to depend on the coupling in a non-monotonic way: it rises above the general relativity value, peaks near 2.02 solar masses at a moderate positive coupling, and then falls steeply as the coupling approaches a critical value at which the structure equations become singular. The two-solar-mass pulsar constraint is satisfied within a finite window of positive couplings. All configurations on the candidate stable branch satisfy causality and remain below the standard general-relativistic compactness and surface-redshift benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [Structural morphology and the gravitational arrow of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27526)
*Julian Barbour,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究零能量、零线动量、零角动量扇区中牛顿N体问题的Janus点结构，发现全局尺度不变聚合度V并非点态单调，而局部六邻居各向异性A6可以揭示具有相同全局V值的中心构型在局部几何组织上存在显著差异，说明全局标量不能唯一确定形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究BKM引力箭头中起核心作用的全局尺度不变标量V是否足以唯一地表征形态学信息。通过比较两个全局V值仅差1.686%的平面N=5000中心构型的局部几何组织，检验全局标量与局部形态之间的对应关系。

Method: 利用两组N=5000粒子的数值粒子坐标数据，以六邻居各向异性A6（0为各向同性，1为一维）量化局部形态，在4≤k≤12的邻域尺度下进行测试，并进行无量纲近距离配对稳健性检验。

Result: 两个构型的V仅相差1.686%，但平均各向异性相差156.8%（0.1331 vs 0.3419）。高V构型中18.1%的粒子满足A6>0.5，而低V构型中为零。各向异性排序在所有测试邻域尺寸下保持一致。

Conclusion: 全局标量V不能唯一确定构型形态，局部关系可观测量是互补的描述层次，为静态形状空间景观与真实Janus点历史中的形态学之间提供了定量桥梁。

Abstract: The Newtonian $N$-body problem in the zero-energy, zero-linear-momentum, and zero-angular-momentum sector provides a time-reversal-invariant setting in which generic complete solutions possess a Janus point and exhibit a gravitational arrow on both branches away from it. The dimensionless variety $V$, a global scale-invariant measure of clustering contrast, is not pointwise monotonic but fluctuates while growing between rising bounds away from the Janus region. Central configurations are critical shapes of the same scale-invariant landscape and therefore provide controlled probes of the structural information encoded by $V$. We investigate this question for two planar $N=5000$ central configurations using the numerical particle-coordinate data. Local morphology is quantified by the six-neighbour anisotropy $A_6$, which ranges from $0$ for an isotropic local environment to $1$ for an effectively one-dimensional one. Although the varieties of the two configurations differ by only $1.686\%$, their mean anisotropies differ by $156.8\%$, from $0.1331$ to $0.3419$. Moreover, $18.1\%$ of the particles in the higher-variety configuration satisfy $A_6>0.5$, whereas none do so in the lower-variety configuration. The anisotropy ordering persists for all tested neighbourhood sizes $4\le k\le12$, and the principal contrast survives a dimensionless close-pair robustness test. For this pair of critical shapes, nearby values of the global variety therefore coexist with markedly different local geometrical organization. Thus the scalar quantity whose long-term behavior characterizes the BKM gravitational arrow does not, by itself, uniquely specify morphology. This identifies local relational observables as a complementary level of description and provides a quantitative bridge between the static shape-space landscape and morphology along genuine Janus-point histories.

</details>


### [169] [Tidal Love numbers of multi-state Boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27587)
*Xin-Lei Zhao,Jun-Ru Chen,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由基态和第一激发态复标量场组成的多态玻色子星（MSBSs）的潮汐洛夫数。在同步和非同步频率条件下，背景解分为单分支和双分支两类。研究发现电极性潮汐洛夫数先为正，在特定参数条件下突变为负值，而磁极性潮汐洛夫数始终为负且绝对值更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多态玻色子星是暗物质和致密天体物理中的重要研究对象，潮汐洛夫数是探测中子星等致密天体内部结构的关键可观测量。此前对单态玻色子星的潮汐洛夫数已有研究，但多态玻色子星（由基态和激发态标量场组成）的潮汐响应尚未被系统探索，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先构造由基态和第一激发态复标量场构成的多态玻色子星背景解，在同步和非同步频率条件下将其分类为单分支和双分支类型，并分析场函数、ADM质量和束缚能等性质。随后对包含稳定解的分支，求解线性扰动方程计算四极（l=2）电极性和磁极性潮汐洛夫数。

Result: 电极性潮汐洛夫数先为正，随后在参数满足 μ̃₁ > 0.891 或 ω̃₀ > 0.777 时突变为负值；参数较小时电极性洛夫数保持正值。磁极性潮汐洛夫数始终为负，且其绝对值小于电极性潮汐洛夫数。

Conclusion: 多态玻色子星的潮汐洛夫数表现出非平凡的符号转变行为，电极性洛夫数从正到负的突变与特定参数阈值相关，这为区分多态玻色子星与中子星及其他致密天体提供了潜在的观测判据，也有助于深化对玻色子星内部结构及引力波信号的认知。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the tidal Love numbers of multi-state boson stars (MSBSs) composed of ground state and first excited state complex scalar fields. Under synchronized and nonsynchronized frequency conditions, the background solutions of MSBSs are classified into single-branch and double-branch types. The field functions, ADM mass, and binding energy of different solutions are discussed. We then calculate the quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) electric and magnetic tidal Love numbers for branches containing stable solutions. Our results show that the electric tidal Love numbers are initially positive and then suddenly transition to negative values. This phenomenon occurs when the parameters satisfy $\tildeμ_1 > 0.891$ or $\tildeω_0 > 0.777$; for smaller values of these parameters, the electric Love numbers remain positive. The magnetic tidal Love numbers are always negative, with absolute values smaller than those of the electric tidal Love numbers.

</details>


### [170] [Constrained thermodynamics and geodesic observables of an effective non-commutative Kerr-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27707)
*H. Hassanabadi,L. A. López,N. Bretón,L. M. Nieto,S. Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究非对易背景下有效Kerr-like黑洞的视界结构、约束热力学及测地线性质。非对易形变通过质量依赖的类电荷项修改径向几何，同时保持测地线方程的可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易时空对旋转黑洞几何与物理性质的影响，特别是视界存在条件、极端零温构型、热力学解释及光子轨道和阴影的修正效应，以理解量子引力修正对黑洞可观测特征的影响。

Method: 通过引入非对易形变参数修改Kerr度规的径向几何，分析视界条件、静止极限面与能量层，区分几何Hawking量与约束态空间中的共轭变量，推导正则系综和巨正则系综的热容，求解光子区域、赤道光环、阴影边界及最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO），并计算不变光子频移。

Result: 确定了视界存在条件及极端零温构型；非对易形变使特征光子轨道向内偏移并减小阴影的整体尺寸；对顺行和逆行ISCO分支均产生向内偏移；揭示了不同系综下局部热行为的差异。

Conclusion: 非对易背景下的Kerr-like黑洞在保持测地线可分离性的同时，其光子轨道、阴影和稳定圆轨道均受到向内收缩的修正，且热力学性质依赖于不同系综的选择，需区分几何Hawking量与约束状态变量。

Abstract: We investigate the horizon structure, constrained thermodynamics, and geodesic properties of an effective Kerr-like black hole in a non-commutative background. Deformation modifies the radial geometry through a mass-dependent charge-like contribution, while preserving the separability of the geodesic equations. We determine the conditions for horizon existence, identify the extremal zero-temperature configuration, and analyze the stationary-limit surfaces and the ergoregion. Special attention is paid to the thermodynamic interpretation of the model, where the geometric Hawking quantities are distinguished from the conjugate variables associated with the constrained state space at fixed non-commutative deformation parameter. The canonical and grand-canonical heat capacities are derived to characterize their ensemble-dependent local thermal behavior. We also obtain the spherical photon region, equatorial light rings, and shadow boundary, showing that the deformation shifts the characteristic photon orbits inwards and reduces the overall size of the shadow. Timelike circular motion is studied through the innermost stable circular orbit, where non-commutative correction produces an inward shift of both the prograde and retrograde branches. Finally, invariant photon frequency shifts are obtained by treating the emitter's orbital direction and the photon's tangential emission direction as independent physical choices.

</details>


### [171] [Degenerate Limits of Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27801)
*Bekir Baytaş,Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量-张量引力理论在哈密顿联络动力学框架下的退化极限，通过空间三标架的局域标度变换实现退化，并推导出退化标量-张量引力理论的约束条件及其物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量-张量引力理论的退化极限，理解在空间三标架退化的情形下联络动力学如何退化，以及退化后各几何量和关系式的变化，从而拓展对该理论极端情形下物理行为的理解。

Method: 采用哈密顿联络动力学（connection dynamics）表述，通过对空间三标架施加局域标度变换（local scaling transformation）来引入退化极限，在此基础上推导所有相关几何量的退化形式，并进一步导出退化标量-张量引力理论的约束条件。

Result: 成功推导出退化标量-张量引力理论在联络动力学表述中的约束方程组，并对这些约束的物理意义进行了讨论。

Conclusion: 通过局域标度变换实现空间三标架的退化，完整推导了退化标量-张量引力理论的约束关系，其物理含义表明该退化框架为理解极端引力情形提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study the degenerate limits of the scalar-tensor theory of gravity in its Hamiltonian connection dynamics formulation. The degenerate limit of the spatial triad is effectuated by a local scaling transformation of the triad, which leads to the degenerate limits of all other geometric quantities and relations in the connection dynamics. We derive the constraints for the degenerate scalar-tensor theory of gravity and discuss their physical implications.

</details>


### [172] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称遥平行引力背景下广义Dirac方程的非相对论极限，通过Foldy–Wouthuysen变换推导出包含自旋–引力、各向异性自旋–动量–引力及潮汐自旋–动量耦合等新相互作用通道的有效哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称遥平行引力中广义旋量联络（包含完整Clifford代数基）所引入的额外非度量性耦合在低能极限下的物理效应，为约束这些额外耦合参数提供理论框架。

Method: 在重合规范下，采用各向同性坐标中的弱场Schwarzschild几何，构造广义Dirac哈密顿量，并执行逐阶Foldy–Wouthuysen变换至1/m²阶，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项；随后对地球弱引力场中的电子进行量级分析以验证逆质量展开截断的合理性。

Result: 得到的块对角化哈密顿量除包含动能、自旋–轨道和Darwin相互作用的引力对应项外，还产生了直接自旋–引力、各向异性自旋–动量–引力以及潮汐自旋–动量耦合等新算子结构。

Conclusion: 广义Dirac方程在对称遥平行背景下产生了涉及费米子自旋、动量及引力场空间导数的新低能相互作用通道，为探索广义旋量联络中额外耦合的唯象约束提供了有效哈密顿量框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>


### [173] [Towards long and accurate numerical relativity waveforms of binary black holes beyond general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27991)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了将谱方法和"fixing-the-equations"方法相结合，能够在超越广义相对论的理论中生成文献中最长的数值相对论波形，具体针对平移对称的标量-高斯-邦尼特引力理论，生成了等质量、无自旋、低偏心率黑洞双星系统的引力波和标量波波形，相位误差小于1弧度（40+引力波周期）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越广义相对论理论中的数值相对论模拟对发展未来的引力波检验引力理论至关重要，但此前缺乏足够长且精确的波形，不足以支撑后牛顿理论与波形模型的精确校准。

Method: 结合谱方法（spectral methods）与"fixing-the-equations"方法，在平移对称的标量-高斯-邦尼特引力（shift-symmetric scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity）中求解等质量、无自旋、降低偏心率（eccentricity-reduced）的黑洞双星系统，并在未来零无穷远处提取引力波与标量波波形。

Result: 生成了文献中最长的超越GR理论波形（40+引力波周期，20+轨道），相位误差不超过约1弧度；该理论下的引力波相位修正与爱因斯坦广义相对论可区分，且合并时间早于GR预言。

Conclusion: 这项工作使替代引力理论的数值相对论方法更接近GR领域的最新水平，为与后牛顿理论的精确比较以及超越GR的波形模型的标定奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Numerical relativity (NR) simulations of compact binaries in theories beyond general relativity (GR) will be pivotal for the continued development of future tests of gravity with gravitational waves (GWs). In this Letter, we show that the combination of spectral methods and the "fixing-the-equations" approach allows us to produce the longest waveforms in the literature for a genuine beyond-GR theory, thus bringing NR methods for alternative theories of gravity closer to the state-of-the-art in GR. For concreteness, we focus on the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity, a theory postulating the existence of an additional dynamical scalar and describing black holes (BHs) different from the Kerr solution. We extract the gravitational and scalar waveforms at future null infinity for equal-mass, nonspinning, eccentricity-reduced BH binaries, and quantify the phase errors to be $\lesssim$ 1 rad after 40+ GW cycles (20+ orbits). We also show that the GW phase corrections in this alternative theory are distinguishable from Einstein's theory and lead to an earlier coalescence time than in GR. Obtaining such waveforms is a stepping stone to perform precise comparisons with Post-Newtonian theory and to calibrate waveform models beyond GR.

</details>


### [174] [High-accuracy drivers to simulate black hole binaries beyond general relativity with the fixing-the-equations approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28003)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Spectre（基于伪谱间断Galerkin方案的数值相对论代码）中实现了"fixing-the-equations"方法，用于在shift-symmetric标量Gauss-Bonnet引力中生成长时间、高精度的引力波波形，取得了在近40个GW周期内相位误差≤O(1)弧度的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论（如sGB引力）中，数值相对论模拟需要长时间、高精度的引力波波形来支持引力波天文学，但标准的约束阻尼和driver方程方法在非广义相对论理论中效果不佳，需要新的技术来处理fully-coupled方程组的（准）平稳解。

Method: 1) 引入一族新的comoving driver方程，利用准圆双星系统的近似对称性来恢复fully-coupled理论的精确（准）平稳解；2) 针对张量driver方程提出更合适的推广方式，避免将张量分量当作标量处理所导致的BH自旋虚假增长问题；3) 结合偏心距约减和Cauchy特征演化（CCE）进行波提取。

Result: 单BH解通过解析预测得到验证；即使在早期旋进的二元BH中，内禀BH量对driver方程的时间尺度相对不敏感；偏心距≤10^-3，在近40个GW周期内获得相位误差≤O(1)弧度的波形，并自然包含记忆贡献。

Conclusion: 本文展示了在sGB引力中通过精心设计的comoving driver方程和张量推广，可以在修正引力理论中实现与广义相对论相当水平的长期高精度数值相对论模拟，为修正引力框架下的引力波模板生成提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We implement the "fixing-the-equations" approach [Phys.Rev.D 96 (2017) 8, 084043] in spectre, an NR code using a pseudo-spectral discontinuous Galerkin scheme, to produce long and accurate NR waveforms in the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) gravity. To achieve this, we introduce a new family of comoving driver equations that exploits the approximate symmetries of quasicircular binary systems and is designed to recover the exact (quasi-)stationary solutions of the fully-coupled theory. We validate our single black hole (BH) solutions against analytic predictions and show that, even for binary BHs in the early inspiral, the intrinsic BH quantities are relatively insensitive to the timescales entering the driver equation. Attention is given to the prescription of driver equations for tensors, for which we give an example of how treating tensor components as scalars can lead to undesired behaviour over long timescales, including spurious growth of the BH spins. A more appropriate generalization to the tensor case is given for the comoving driver, which is shown to avoid these issues. Overall, our implementation leverages state-of-the-art methods for eccentricity reduction and wave extraction with Cauchy Characteristic Evolution to simulate systems with eccentricity $\lesssim 10^{-3}$. We obtain waveforms with phase errors $\lesssim \mathcal{O}(1) \, \mathrm{rad}$ over almost 40 GW-cycles, which naturally incorporate memory contributions.

</details>


### [175] [Quartic Scalar Clouds on Fixed Kerr Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28044)
*Hendrik Mennenga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究固定Kerr和Schwarzschild背景上的复标量场非线性驻波云，发现满足同步条件ω=mΩ_H的有限振幅Q云，并分析了其在参数平面上的存在区域，包括可延续到Ω_H=0的静态解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定Kerr和Schwarzschild背景下，已有研究主要集中在线性标量云，而带无界标量势的非线性云（Q云）尚缺乏系统研究，特别是其同步条件、存在区域以及静态极限的探索具有重要意义。

Method: 在固定Kerr和Schwarzschild时空背景上，对具有无界标量势的复标量场进行数值求解，寻找满足同步条件ω=mΩ_H的有限振幅Q云解，并系统地扫描(Ω_H, M_K)参数平面确定存在区域，同时以类轴子余弦势作为对比验证。

Result: 在Kerr背景上找到了有限振幅Q云，满足同步条件ω=mΩ_H；这些解占据(Ω_H, M_K)平面上的二维区域，且当标量场振幅趋于零时退化为对应的线性云。四次势情形可延续到Ω_H=0，此时同步频率和Noether荷均消失，解变为固定Schwarzschild背景上的静态非线性标量云。此外还计算了归一化类轴子余弦势的存在域。

Conclusion: 非线性Q云是线性云的有限振幅推广，其存在区域覆盖更广的参数空间，并在静态极限下与Schwarzschild背景上的非线性静态云相连，表明非线性效应扩展了黑子超辐射束缚态的存在范围。

Abstract: We study stationary nonlinear clouds of a complex scalar field on fixed Kerr and Schwarzschild backgrounds, with an unbounded scalar potential. On Kerr backgrounds we find finite-amplitude Q-clouds satisfying the synchronisation condition $ω=mΩ_H$. They occupy two-dimensional regions of the ($Ω_H,M_K$) plane and reduce to the corresponding linear clouds as the scalar field amplitude tends to zero. The quartic domain can continue to $Ω_H=0$, where both the synchronised frequency and the Noether charge vanish and the solutions become static nonlinear scalar clouds on a fixed Schwarzschild background. We also compute the existence domain for a normalised, axion-inspired cosine potential.

</details>


### [176] [Signatures of kinetic gravity braiding in cosmological probes of the gravitational field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28207)
*Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz,Farbod Hassani,Julian Adamek,Emilio Bellini,Martin Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用相对论N体代码KGB-evolution，研究了动能引力布拉廷（KGB）模型在沿过去光锥的相对论宇宙学探针中的观测特征。研究发现标量场与度规的导数耦合会修正引力势的振幅和时间演化，产生从百分之几到几十百分比的尺度依赖偏差，其中ISW-RS信号的分数响应最大，弱引力透镜在ℓ~10²-10³处出现高达10-12%的增强，非线性效应在小尺度上不可忽视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动能引力布拉廷（KGB）模型是修改引力理论的重要候选者，但现有研究大多基于线性微扰理论，缺乏沿光锥的完全相对论性观测预言。理解KGB模型在相对论宇宙学探针（如弱引力透镜、ISW-RS效应等）中的非线性观测特征，对于利用下一代巡天数据区分不同引力理论至关重要。

Method: 使用相对论N体代码KGB-evolution生成沿过去光锥的光锥输出，计算多个直接探测引力场的可观测量，包括弱引力透镜会聚、Shapiro时间延迟、ISW-RS效应和引力红移。构建全天图和角功率谱，并与k-essence模型和线性微扰理论预言进行比较分析。

Result: 标量场与度规的导数耦合同时修正了引力势的振幅和时间演化，产生从几个百分点到几十个百分点的尺度依赖偏差。ISW-RS信号表现出最大的分数响应：在ISW主导区域，Weyl势衰减减慢抑制了KGB信号，而非线性演化在高多极处逆转这一趋势。弱引力透镜在ℓ~10²-10³处相对k-essence预测显示出高达10-12%的增强。线性理论能准确描述大尺度行为，但非线性效应在小尺度（尤其是对ISW-RS和会聚度）变得重要。

Conclusion: KGB模型在相对论性探针中产生独特的可观测信号，弱引力透镜和ISW-RS效应是约束该模型的关键互补探针。可靠的KGB理论预言必须包含非线性效应，特别是在小尺度上，这为下一代宇宙学巡天区分KGB与k-essence模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the observational signatures of kinetic gravity braiding (KGB) models in relativistic cosmological probes constructed along the past light cone. Using the relativistic $N$-body code KGB-evolution, we generate light-cone outputs and compute several observables that directly probe the gravitational field, including weak gravitational lensing convergence, Shapiro time delay, the integrated Sachs-Wolfe and Rees-Sciama (ISW-RS) effects, and gravitational redshift. Full-sky maps and angular power spectra of these quantities are constructed and compared with $k$-essence models and predictions from linear perturbation theory. We find that the derivative coupling between the scalar field and the metric modifies both the amplitude and the time evolution of the gravitational potentials, producing scale-dependent deviations ranging from a few percent to tens of percent. In particular, the ISW-RS signal exhibits the largest fractional response, as the slower decay of the Weyl potential suppresses the KGB signal in the ISW-dominated regime, whereas nonlinear evolution reverses this trend at higher multipoles, producing differences of tens of percent relative to $k$-essence. Weak gravitational lensing also provides a strong complementary probe and, for the model considered here, exhibits clear deviations from the $k$-essence prediction at small scales with enhancements up to $\sim 10$-$12\%$ at multipoles $\ell \sim 10^2$-$10^3$. Our results show that linear perturbation theory accurately describes the large-scale behaviour, while nonlinear effects become important at smaller scales, particularly for the ISW-RS signal and, more moderately, for the convergence, and must therefore be included for reliable theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [177] [Homogeneous and Isotropic Linearized Gravity as a Caldeira--Leggett System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28209)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了均匀各向同性线性化引力（含最小耦合自由度）与Caldeira-Leggett开放系统模型之间的等价性，从而将引力问题纳入系统-储库形式体系，推导出度规扰动的广义Langevin方程，并利用Laplace方法分析相关动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将引力理论中的线性化扰动问题与成熟的开放量子系统形式体系（Caldeira-Leggett模型）建立联系，以借用后者的丰富数学工具和物理洞察来研究引力扰动的非平衡动力学、退相干及随机引力行为。

Method: 首先证明均匀各向同性线性化引力（含最小耦合自由度）与Caldeira-Leggett模型的数学等价性；然后通过消去储库自由度，将线性化引力方程约化为度规扰动的广义Langevin方程，环境效应通过谱密度加以浓缩；基于此等价性进一步开展约化量子化和随机引力动力学分析，并运用Laplace方法解析动力学。

Result: 成功建立了线性化引力与Caldeira-Leggett开放系统模型的等价关系，将引力扰动方程约化为包含谱密度的广义Langevin方程，并提供了一种自然框架进行约化量子化与随机引力动力学研究；通过Laplace方法获得的动力学分析结果可推广至其他开放系统情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为引力扰动的开放系统研究提供了新的范式，架起了引力物理学与量子耗散理论之间的桥梁，为后续研究引力系统的量子退相干、引力噪声及随机引力波等课题奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish the equivalence between homogeneous and isotropic linearized gravity with minimally coupled degrees of freedom and the Caldeira--Leggett model of open systems. This connects gravitation to an established system-plus-reservoir formalism, which we use throughout. Eliminating the reservoir reduces the equations of linearized gravity to a generalized Langevin equation for the metric perturbation, with the environmental influence condensed in the spectral density. The equivalence also yields a natural setting for reduced quantization of the perturbation and stochastic gravitational dynamics. We analyze the resulting dynamics with Laplace methods, extending results presented in other open-system settings.

</details>


### [178] [Logarithmic corrections to black hole entropy from minimum-assumptions discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28249)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Ana Alonso-Serrano,Ernesto Contreras,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在不可知论和最小假设下的通用模型，通过将视界离散化为普朗克尺度的粗粒化不可区分几何结构单元，纯粹利用统计组合计数来唯一确定熵面积律及其固定的对数修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子引力/黑洞熵研究中，如何仅基于最少的假设、在不可知论的框架下唯一推导出熵面积律以及其中固定的对数修正项，是一个核心问题。已有模型各假设不同，需要找到统一且最小化的假设条件。

Method: 将视界离散化为普朗克尺度的粗粒化单元，这些单元代表不可区分的几何结构；通过统计组合计数的数学方法推导熵的形式，从而得到面积律和对数修正项，并对假设条件的作用及其与已知固定对数贡献模型的关系进行对比分析。

Result: 在不可知论和最小假设下，唯一地得到了熵面积律以及固定的对数修正项，并阐明了各假设条件在推导中的作用及与现有模型的一致性。

Conclusion: 该模型以最少的假设条件，仅通过组合计数即可唯一确定黑洞熵的面积律及其固定对数修正项，为理解量子引力中熵的微观起源提供了通用且简洁的框架。

Abstract: We introduce here a general model, under agnostic and minimum assumptions, to uniquely find the entropy area law with a corresponding fixed logarithmic correction term. In this approach, the horizon is discretized into generic Planck-scale cells representing coarse-grained indistinguishable geometric structures, and it is just the statistical combinatorial counting that determines the form of the entropy terms. We highlight the role of the assumptions and their comparison with known models providing fixed logarithmic contributions to the entropy.

</details>


### [179] [Ab Initio Real-Time Gravitational-Wave Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28265)
*David Yallup,Metha Prathaban,James Alvey,Thomas C. K. Ng,Thibeau Wouters,Nikhil Sarin,Will Handley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种GPU原生的嵌套采样内核（基于Slice-within-Gibbs结构），用于加速引力波参数的快速估计。在单GPU上处理三探测器网络的长时双中子星信号仅需12分钟中位数，四个设备分片部署可缩短至5分钟；利用外差压缩数据后中位墙钟时间降至89秒，GW170817信号的进动自旋与潮汐波形推断仅需约2分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波参数估计通常计算成本极高，传统随机采样方法难以满足实时推断需求。作者希望探索在现代GPU硬件上，将基线随机采样技术推向实时引力波参数估计的可能边界，从而大幅缩短推断时间。

Method: 构建GPU原生嵌套采样内核，采用Slice-within-Gibbs（SwiG）结构实现快速混合，并研究如何在现代GPU硬件上最大化基线随机采样技术的性能。同时利用外差法对数据进行压缩以进一步降低计算负担，并支持多GPU分片部署。

Result: 在单GPU上，对LIGO和Virgo三探测器网络的长时双中子星完整未压缩数据可实现良好校准的后验推断，中位数时间12分钟；四设备分片时为5分钟。外差压缩后中位数墙钟时间降至89秒（短于数据段本身时长），GW170817的进动自旋和潮汐波形推断约2分钟完成。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合GPU硬件加速与高效的采样结构，从无信息先验出发、使用完整物理波形计算的随机采样技术可以接近实时引力波参数估计的目标，为下一代引力波推断工具奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a specialised GPU-native nested sampling kernel targeting rapid parameter estimation for gravitational wave inference problems. Building upon a Slice-within-Gibbs (SwiG) structure for rapid mixing, we investigate how far we can push baseline stochastic sampling techniques on modern GPU hardware. We demonstrate that for typical long-duration binary neutron star signals observed by the LIGO and Virgo detectors, we can achieve well calibrated posterior inference on the full uncompressed data of a three detector network in a median of twelve minutes on a single GPU. This falls to five minutes when sharded across four devices. Utilising heterodyning to compress the data reduces the median wall time across an injection campaign to 89 seconds -- less than the length of the segment itself -- and enables inference with precessing spin, tidal waveforms on GW170817 in around two minutes. This pushes stochastic sampling techniques using full physical waveform calculations, launched from an uninformed prior state, towards real-time gravitational wave parameter estimation.

</details>


### [180] [Post-Newtonian Roche-Lobe-Overflow Prescription for Compact Binary Mass Transfer and the Corresponding Gravitational Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28315)
*Shuai Zhang,Jie Yang,Zi-Han Zhang,Shenghua Yu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从一阶后牛顿流体动力学方程出发，推导了后牛顿洛希势和质量转移描述，并将其纳入双星动力学，计算了对运动方程、引力波通量和波形的修正，应用于超致密双星系统后发现质量转移会引起长观测时间上的引力波相位漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双星系统中的质量转移是许多天体物理现象（包括致密相互作用双星演化）的核心，但以往的质量转移模型主要基于牛顿框架，缺乏后牛顿修正，需要建立更精确的理论框架来评估质量转移对引力波观测的影响。

Method: 从旋转坐标系中的一阶后牛顿流体动力学方程出发，推导一阶后牛顿洛希势，构建相应的洛希瓣溢出质量转移方案后牛顿形式；将组分质量的时间依赖性纳入双星动力学，计算运动方程、引力波能量和角动量通量以及远区偏振波形的后牛顿修正；并将模型应用于代表性超致密双星系统。

Result: 发现质量转移对致密双星的动力学演化具有重要作用；对引力波观测而言，其主要效应表现为长时间观测累积的相位漂移（世俗相位漂移）。

Conclusion: 后牛顿质量转移模型对理解致密相互作用双星的演化和引力波观测具有重要意义，质量转移导致的引力波相位漂移需要在长期观测中加以考虑。

Abstract: Mass transfer in binary systems is central to many astrophysical phenomena, including the evolution of compact interacting binaries. Starting from the first post-Newtonian hydrodynamic equations in the corotating frame, we derive the first post-Newtonian Roche potential and construct the corresponding post-Newtonian form of the Roche lobe overflow mass transfer prescription. We then include the time dependence of the component masses in the binary dynamics and compute the associated corrections to the equations of motion, gravitational-wave energy and angular-momentum fluxes, and far-zone polarization waveforms. Finally, we apply the model to representative ultracompact binary systems. We find that mass transfer can play an important role in the dynamical evolution of compact binaries. For gravitational-wave observations, its main effect appears as a secular phase drift accumulated over long observation times.

</details>


### [181] [The BGV Theorem and the Null Convergence Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28412)
*William H. Kinney*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零收敛条件（NCC）与Borde-Guth-Vilenkin（BGV）定理之间的关系。研究发现，在无剪切的膨胀时空（正交叶状结构、无涡度）中，若NCC成立且空间曲率非正，则BGV定理成立；但在存在剪切或非测地线穿透的情况下，BGV定理不再由NCC和曲率条件保证，需要更一般化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGV定理在宇宙学中常被用于论证时空的过去不完备性（如永恒膨胀模型需要初始奇点），但其成立的充分条件与零收敛条件（NCC）之间的精确关系尚未被充分厘清。本研究旨在明确NCC和空间曲率条件在何种情况下能够保证BGV定理成立，以及在剪切、加速等更复杂情形下需要什么样的修正条件。

Method: 作者从数学上分析了BGV构造中局域膨胀的表达式。首先考虑膨胀时空沿类时测地线矢量场正交叶状结构且剪切和涡度为零的情形，证明NCC加上非正空间曲率（³R≤0）可推出BGV定理。随后推广到存在剪切或非测地线穿透的一般情形，揭示局域膨胀除标量膨胀外还包含剪切和加速与局域空间单位矢量的缩并项，并基于此推导出适用于渐近过去de Sitter永恒膨胀的更一般化BGV条件。

Result: 主要结果表明：(1) 在无剪切、无涡度、正交叶状结构且空间曲率非正的膨胀时空中，NCC足以保证BGV定理成立；(2) 存在剪切或非测地线穿透时，局域膨胀包含剪切和加速贡献，NCC与曲率条件不再充分；(3) 作者给出了适用于渐近过去de Sitter永恒膨胀（存在共动曲率扰动）的更一般化BGV条件。

Conclusion: BGV定理的有效性与时空的几何结构（剪切、加速、空间曲率）以及收敛条件密切相关。在一般情形下需要更精细的充分条件，作者提出的推广版本对理解永恒膨胀模型中过去时空的（不）完备性具有重要含义。

Abstract: We examine the relationship between the Null Convergence Condition (NCC) and the Borde-Guth-Vilenkin (BGV) Theorem. We first show that, for an expanding spacetime foliated orthogonally to a timelike geodesic congruence with vanishing shear and vorticity, the BGV Theorem follows when the NCC holds and the spatial curvature is non-positive, ${}^3\mathcal{R} \leq 0$. The situation becomes more complex in the presence of shear, or non-geodesic threading of the spacetime. In these cases, the local expansion that enters the BGV construction depends not only on the local scalar expansion, but acquires terms given by the contraction of the shear and acceleration with locally defined spatial unit vectors. In the general case, null convergence and non-positive curvature are no longer sufficient to guarantee that the BGV Theorem holds. We discuss the result in the context of eternal inflation in the presence of comoving curvature perturbations and state a more general version of the BGV condition relevant to asymptotically past-de Sitter eternal inflation.

</details>


### [182] [Dynamics of compact binary systems in massive scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28448)
*Iris van Gemeren,Tanja Hinderer,Stefan Vandoren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在巨质量标量-高斯-博内特（massive sGB）理论框架下，利用后牛顿（PN）近似计算了双非自旋黑洞系统的动力学，首次在1PN阶引入了标量质量与高曲率耦合的修正项，并发现该修正会使束缚能略微减小，且对高质量比和高偏心率系统影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论在高曲率强场区域可能存在高阶曲率修正，宇称不变的二次修正可由标量-高斯-博内特理论描述。给标量场赋予质量是自然的，会引入新的物理尺度和新现象，但目前缺乏对该情形下双黑洞系统动力学的详细研究。

Method: 采用后牛顿（PN）近似方法，计算非自旋双黑洞系统在巨质量sGB理论中的运动方程、质心变换和束缚能。推导对一般标量质量有效，最终显式表达式假设标量质量远小于双星系统总质量，并展开到该比值的二次阶。计算涵盖圆轨道和偏心轨道直至1PN阶。

Result: 首次在1PN阶包含了高曲率与标量质量耦合的修正项。标量质量对规范不变的束缚能的修正包含同号和反号项，在微扰极限下整体贡献为反号，使束缚能略微减小。效应在高质量比和大偏心率双星系统中最为显著。

Conclusion: 该工作为巨质量sGB理论中的双黑洞系统提供了1PN阶完整动力学描述，标量质量修正使束缚能略减，效应在高质量比和高偏心率系统中最大。这些方法和结果为后续计算此类系统产生的引力波提供了基础。

Abstract: Inspiraling binary systems of compact objects probe gravity in strong-field regimes, thereby exploring potential higher curvature corrections to General Relativity. Parity-invariant quadratic corrections can be described by scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) theory, which involves a scalar field dynamically coupled to curvature scalars and can give rise to scalar condensates around black holes. Considering a mass for the scalar field is natural and leads to new phenomenology related to this additional scale. We compute the dynamics of a binary system of nonspinning black holes in massive sGB using the post-Newtonian (PN) approximation. We obtain solutions for the equations of motion, center-of-mass transformation, and binding energy for circular and eccentric orbits up to 1PN order, where for the first time the higher curvature coupled to scalar mass corrections are included. While most of our calculations are valid for generic scalar masses, the final explicit expressions assume that the mass is small compared to the total mass of the binary and expand to quadratic order in this ratio. We show that the scalar mass corrections to the gauge-invariant binding energy come with same and opposite sign order terms, contributing an overall opposite sign contribution in the perturbative limit, decreasing the binding energy slightly. The effects are largest for binary systems with high mass ratio and large eccentricity. Our methods and results will also be useful as a basis for computing the gravitational waves sourced by such systems.

</details>
